local Library = nil
local function Notify(text, duration)
    if Library and Library.Notify then
        pcall(function() Notify(tostring(text), duration or 3) end)
    else
        pcall(function()
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Soul.Tech",
                Text = tostring(text),
                Duration = duration or 3
            })
        end)
    end
end

local Utility = {
    NewConnection = function(signal, callback)
        local success, conn = pcall(function() return signal:Connect(callback) end)
        return success and conn or { Disconnect = function() end }
    end,
    IsVisible = function(startPos, endPos, ignoreList)
        local parts = Camera:GetPartsObscuringTarget({startPos, endPos}, ignoreList or {})
        return #parts == 0
    end,
    WorldToScreen = function(pos)
        return Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
    end,
    GetTool = function()
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    end
}
local CustomMath = {
    CFrameToOffset = function(Origin, Target)
        local ActualOrigin = Origin * CFrame.new(0, -0.25, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, -1, 0)
        return ActualOrigin:ToObjectSpace(Target):inverse()
    end,
    RandomVector3 = function(minVal, maxVal)
        return Vector3.new(math.random(minVal, maxVal), math.random(minVal, maxVal), math.random(minVal, maxVal))
    end
}
local ConnectionsStorage = {}
local DhLibrary = {}
function DhLibrary:Create(className, properties)
    local inst = Instance.new(className)
    if properties then
        for k, v in pairs(properties) do
            if k ~= "Parent" then
                pcall(function() inst[k] = v end)
            end
        end
        if properties.Parent then
            inst.Parent = properties.Parent
        end
    end
    return inst
end
function DhLibrary:AddConnection(name, conn)
    if ConnectionsStorage[name] then
        pcall(function() ConnectionsStorage[name]:Disconnect() end)
    end
    ConnectionsStorage[name] = conn
end
function DhLibrary:RemoveConnection(name)
    if ConnectionsStorage[name] then
        pcall(function() ConnectionsStorage[name]:Disconnect() end)
        ConnectionsStorage[name] = nil
    end
end
function DhLibrary:PlayAnimation(character, animId, fade, weight, speed)
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        local animObj = Instance.new("Animation")
        animObj.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animId)
        local track = hum:LoadAnimation(animObj)
        track:Play(fade or 0.1, weight or 1, speed or 1)
        return track
    end
end
function DhLibrary:StopAnimation(character, animId)
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track.Animation and string.find(tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId), tostring(animId)) then
                track:Stop()
            end
        end
    end
end
function DhLibrary:IsAnimPlaying(character, animId)
    local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track.Animation and string.find(tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId), tostring(animId)) and track.IsPlaying then
                return true
            end
        end
    end
    return false
end
function DhLibrary:NoVelocity(character)
    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            part.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
            part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end
end
function DhLibrary:CanCollide(character, bool)
    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            part.CanCollide = bool
        end
    end
end
function DhLibrary:GetChar(name)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    if name and name ~= "" then
        return char:WaitForChild(name, 5)
    end
    return char
end

local getrawmetatable = getrawmetatable or debug.getrawmetatable
local setrawmetatable = setrawmetatable or debug.setrawmetatable
local hookmetamethod = hookmetamethod or function(obj, meth, fn)
    local mt = getrawmetatable(obj)
    local old = mt[meth]
    setreadonly(mt, false)
    mt[meth] = fn
    setreadonly(mt, true)
    return old
end
local hookfunction = hookfunction or replaceclosure or function(f, n) return f end
local newcclosure = newcclosure or function(f) return f end
local checkcaller = checkcaller or function() return false end
local islclosure = islclosure or function() return true end
local isourclosure = isourclosure or function() return false end
local getnamecallmethod = getnamecallmethod or function() return "" end
local setnamecallmethod = setnamecallmethod or function(m) end
local setreadonly = setreadonly or function(t, v) end

pcall(function()
    for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(LocalPlayer.Idled)) do
        if conn.Disable then
            conn:Disable()
        elseif conn.Disconnect then
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
    end
end)
pcall(function()
    local clientScript = LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("Client")
    if clientScript and clientScript:IsA("LocalScript") then
        local gc = getgc and getgc(true) or {}
        for _, obj in pairs(gc) do
            if type(obj) == "function" and islclosure(obj) and not isourclosure(obj) then
                local info = debug.getinfo(obj)
                if info and info.source and (string.find(info.source, "Client") or string.find(info.source, "Anti") or string.find(info.source, "Security")) then
                    hookfunction(obj, newcclosure(function(...) return end))
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local TargetPlr = nil
local TargetPart = nil
local function isPlayerAlive(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return hum and hum.Health > 0
end
local function isPlayerKnocked(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    local be = player.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
    if be then
        local ko = be:FindFirstChild("K.O") or be:FindFirstChild("KO")
        if ko and ko.Value then return true end
        local dead = be:FindFirstChild("Dead")
        if dead and dead.Value then return true end
    end
    return false
end
local function isPlayerGrabbed(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    local be = player.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
    if be and be:FindFirstChild("Grabbed") and be.Grabbed.Value ~= nil then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local function isPlayerBehindWall(origin, part, ignoreList)
    local ray = Ray.new(origin, (part.Position - origin).Unit * (part.Position - origin).Magnitude)
    local hit, _ = Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray, ignoreList or { LocalPlayer.Character, Camera })
    if hit and not hit:IsDescendantOf(part.Parent) then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local function isPlayerVisible(part)
    if not part then return false end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local origin = Camera.CFrame.Position
    local ignore = { char, Camera }
    return not isPlayerBehindWall(origin, part, ignore)
end
local function isPlayerInAir(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return false end
    local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum and hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
        return true
    end
    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and math.abs(hrp.Velocity.Y) > 5 then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local function getTargetHitPart(player, isAir, settingsTable)
    if not player or not player.Character then return nil end
    local partName = settingsTable.HitPart or "Head"
    if isAir and settingsTable.AirCheck and settingsTable.AirPart then
        partName = settingsTable.AirPart
    end
    if partName == "Random" then
        local parts = { "Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "LowerTorso" }
        partName = parts[math.random(1, #parts)]
    end
    return player.Character:FindFirstChild(partName) or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function getPingPrediction()
    local pingValue = 50
    pcall(function()
        local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local net = stats.Network
        if net and net.ServerStatsItem and net.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"] then
            pingValue = net.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
        end
    end)
    for i = 1, #pingSets do
        if pingValue <= pingSets[i].Ping then
            return pingSets[i].Pred
        end
    end
    return 0.145
end
local function getClosestPlayerToCursor(fovRadius, checkWall, checkFF, checkTeam, checkAlive)
    local closest = nil
    local shortestDist = fovRadius or math.huge
    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            local valid = true
            if checkAlive and (not isPlayerAlive(plr) or isPlayerKnocked(plr)) then
                valid = false
            end
            if checkTeam and plr.Team == LocalPlayer.Team then
                valid = false
            end
            if checkFF and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then
                valid = false
            end
            if valid then
                local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if head then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
                    if onScreen then
                        if not checkWall or isPlayerVisible(head) then
                            local dist = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
                            if dist < shortestDist then
                                shortestDist = dist
                                closest = plr
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end
local function toggleLock(specificPlr)
    if TargetPlr then
        TargetPlr = nil
        TargetPart = nil
        return false
    else
        local target = specificPlr or getClosestPlayerToCursor(
            SoulSettings.Camlock.CheckWall and 500 or math.huge,
            SoulSettings.Camlock.CheckWall,
            SoulSettings.Camlock.CheckForcefield,
            SoulSettings.Camlock.CheckTeam,
            SoulSettings.Camlock.CheckAlive
        )
        if target and target.Character then
            TargetPlr = target
            local isAir = isPlayerInAir(target)
            TargetPart = getTargetHitPart(target, isAir, SoulSettings.Camlock)
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
end
local function CreateMobileButton(name, defaultPos, onClickCallback)
    local screenGui = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("SoulTech_MobileGui")
    if not screenGui then
        screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        screenGui.Name = "SoulTech_MobileGui"
        screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        pcall(function() screenGui.Parent = CoreGui end)
        if not screenGui.Parent then screenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    end
    local existingBtn = screenGui:FindFirstChild(name)
    if existingBtn then existingBtn:Destroy() end
    local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    button.Name = name
    button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 80)
    button.Position = defaultPos or UDim2.new(0.85, -40, 0.5, -40)
    button.Image = "rbxassetid://11351620343"
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 25)
    button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.AutoButtonColor = false
    button.Parent = screenGui
    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = button
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    stroke.Parent = button
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 2)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = name:gsub("Button", "")
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.Parent = button
    local isDragging = false
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPos = nil
    button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            isDragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = button.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    isDragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if isDragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            button.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale,
                startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale,
                startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
    local isActive = false
    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if onClickCallback then
            isActive = onClickCallback()
            if isActive then
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 255)
                stroke.Thickness = 3
                button.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 255)
            else
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
                stroke.Thickness = 2
                button.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end
        end
    end)
    return button
end

local function getBestShopForGun(gunName)
    for _, shop in ipairs(DaHoodShops) do
        if shop.Guns then
            for _, g in ipairs(shop.Guns) do
                if g == gunName then
                    return shop
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function calculateTrajectory(origin, targetPos, velocity, gravity)
    local g = gravity or 196.2
    local diff = targetPos - origin
    local flatDiff = Vector3.new(diff.X, 0, diff.Z)
    local flatDist = flatDiff.Magnitude
    local timeToTarget = flatDist / velocity
    local drop = 0.5 * g * (timeToTarget ^ 2)
    return origin + (diff.Unit * velocity * timeToTarget) + Vector3.new(0, drop, 0)
end
end
do
local AdvancedPrediction = {}
AdvancedPrediction.Matrices = {}
for ping = 1, 1000 do
    local basePred = 0.100 + (ping * 0.0005)
    local highVelocityComp = (ping / 1000) * 1.15
    local groundComp = 0.02 + (ping * 0.0001)
    local airComp = 0.05 + (ping * 0.0002)
    local resolverFactor = 1.0 + (ping * 0.001)
    AdvancedPrediction.Matrices[ping] = {
        Standard = basePred,
        HighVelocity = basePred + highVelocityComp,
        Ground = basePred - groundComp,
        Air = basePred + airComp,
        ResolverMultiplier = resolverFactor,
        SmoothLerp = math.clamp(1 - (ping * 0.0012), 0.05, 0.95),
        FalloffDistance = 150 + (ping * 0.5),
        LeadTicks = math.floor(ping / 15) + 2
    }
end
function AdvancedPrediction.GetSettings(ping)
    local p = math.clamp(math.floor(ping or 50), 1, 1000)
    return AdvancedPrediction.Matrices[p]
end
function AdvancedPrediction.PredictPosition(origin, targetPart, velocity, acceleration, ping, mode)
    local matrix = AdvancedPrediction.GetSettings(ping)
    local currentPos = targetPart.Position
    local predTime = matrix.Standard
    if mode == "HighVelocity" then
        predTime = matrix.HighVelocity
    elseif mode == "Ground" then
        predTime = matrix.Ground
    elseif mode == "Air" then
        predTime = matrix.Air
    end
    local vel = velocity or Vector3.zero
    local acc = acceleration or Vector3.zero
    local predicted = currentPos + (vel * predTime) + (0.5 * acc * (predTime ^ 2))
    return predicted
end

function AdvancedPrediction.GetPingComp(ping)
    local rounded = math.clamp(math.floor(ping / 5) * 5, 5, 500)
    return PingCompensationMatrix[rounded] or { Multiplier = 1.0, BasePred = 0.135, VelocityDamping = 1.0 }
end

local FastTrigLUT = {}
for i = 0, 360 do
    local r = math.rad(i)
    FastTrigLUT[i] = { Sin = math.sin(r), Cos = math.cos(r), Tan = math.tan(r) }
end
function FastTrigLUT.SinDeg(deg)
    local d = math.floor(deg % 360)
    return FastTrigLUT[d] and FastTrigLUT[d].Sin or math.sin(math.rad(deg))
end
function FastTrigLUT.CosDeg(deg)
    local d = math.floor(deg % 360)
    return FastTrigLUT[d] and FastTrigLUT[d].Cos or math.cos(math.rad(deg))
end
end
do
local AdvancedESP3D = {}
AdvancedESP3D.RigBonesR15 = {
    {"Head", "UpperTorso"},
    {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
    {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"},
    {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
    {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
    {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
    {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"},
    {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
    {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"},
    {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
    {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
    {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
    {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"},
    {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"}
}
AdvancedESP3D.RigBonesR6 = {
    {"Head", "Torso"},
    {"Torso", "Left Arm"},
    {"Torso", "Right Arm"},
    {"Torso", "Left Leg"},
    {"Torso", "Right Leg"}
}
function AdvancedESP3D.DrawSkeleton(character, bonesStorage, color, thickness)
    if not character then return end
    local isR15 = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") ~= nil
    local bonesList = isR15 and AdvancedESP3D.RigBonesR15 or AdvancedESP3D.RigBonesR6
    for idx, bonePair in ipairs(bonesList) do
        local part1 = character:FindFirstChild(bonePair[1])
        local part2 = character:FindFirstChild(bonePair[2])
        local line = bonesStorage[idx]
        if not line then
            line = Drawing.new("Line")
            line.Thickness = thickness or 1.5
            line.ZIndex = 2
            bonesStorage[idx] = line
        end
        if part1 and part2 then
            local p1, on1 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part1.Position)
            local p2, on2 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part2.Position)
            if on1 and on2 then
                line.From = Vector2.new(p1.X, p1.Y)
                line.To = Vector2.new(p2.X, p2.Y)
                line.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                line.Visible = true
            else
                line.Visible = false
            end
        else
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end
local MaterialChamsProperties = {
    ["ForceField"] = { Material = Enum.Material.ForceField, Transparency = 0.4 },
    ["Neon"] = { Material = Enum.Material.Neon, Transparency = 0.2 },
    ["Glass"] = { Material = Enum.Material.Glass, Transparency = 0.5 },
    ["SmoothPlastic"] = { Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic, Transparency = 0.3 },
    ["Foil"] = { Material = Enum.Material.Foil, Transparency = 0.3 }
}
function AdvancedESP3D.ApplyMaterialChams(character, chamType, color)
    if not character then return end
    local prop = MaterialChamsProperties[chamType] or MaterialChamsProperties["ForceField"]
    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            part.Material = prop.Material
            part.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 255)
            part.Transparency = prop.Transparency
        end
    end
end
local ColorPalettes = {
    ["SoulTech Purple"] = Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 255),
    ["Cyberpunk Cyan"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
    ["Neon Pink"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 20, 147),
    ["Toxic Lime"] = Color3.fromRGB(50, 205, 50),
    ["Blood Red"] = Color3.fromRGB(220, 20, 60),
    ["Sunset Orange"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0),
    ["Deep Gold"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0),
    ["Electric Blue"] = Color3.fromRGB(30, 144, 255),
    ["Ghost White"] = Color3.fromRGB(248, 248, 255),
    ["Abyss Black"] = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 20)
}
end

do
local function createHitSound()
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.Parent = localPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
    sound.SoundId = SoulTech.HitEffects.HitSoundID
    sound.Volume = SoulTech.HitEffects.HitSoundVolume
    sound:Play()
    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        sound:Destroy()
    end)
end
local function SetRigTransparency(clone, trans)
    for _, v in pairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
            v.Transparency = trans
        end
    end
end
local function SetRigCollisionFalse(clone)
    for _, v in pairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
            v.CanCollide = false
        end
    end
end
local function SetRigColor(clone, color)
    for _, v in pairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") or v:IsA("MeshPart") then
            v.Color = color
        end
    end
end
end
do
local AuraColors = {
    Safe = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
}
local function AttachAuraSafe(character)
    local torso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not torso then return end
    for _, child in ipairs(torso:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "AuraSafe" then child:Destroy() end
    end
    local container = Instance.new("Folder")
    container.Name = "AuraSafe"
    container.Parent = torso
    local pointLight = Instance.new("PointLight")
    pointLight.Range = 5
    pointLight.Brightness = 3
    pointLight.Color = AuraColors.Safe
    pointLight.Enabled = true
    pointLight.Shadows = false
    pointLight.Parent = container
    local glowAttach = Instance.new("Attachment")
    glowAttach.Name = "Glow"
    glowAttach.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)
    glowAttach.Parent = container
    local particleGlow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    particleGlow.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    particleGlow.Brightness = 1
    particleGlow.Color = ColorSequence.new(AuraColors.Safe)
    particleGlow.Drag = 0
    particleGlow.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Back
    particleGlow.Enabled = true
    particleGlow.FlipbookFramerate = NumberRange.new(1, 1)
    particleGlow.FlipbookLayout = Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.None
    particleGlow.FlipbookMode = Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot
    particleGlow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 1)
    particleGlow.LightEmission = 1
    particleGlow.LightInfluence = 1
    particleGlow.LockedToPart = true
    particleGlow.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera
    particleGlow.Rate = 4
    particleGlow.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
    particleGlow.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
    particleGlow.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
    particleGlow.ShapeInOut = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Outward
    particleGlow.ShapePartial = 1
    particleGlow.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
    particleGlow.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 4.25), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 3.375)})
    particleGlow.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.001, 0.001)
    particleGlow.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    particleGlow.Squash = NumberSequence.new(0, 0)
    particleGlow.Texture = "rbxassetid://1075864321"
    particleGlow.TimeScale = 1
    particleGlow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    particleGlow.VelocityInheritance = 0
    particleGlow.WindAffectsDrag = false
    particleGlow.ZOffset = -1
    particleGlow.Parent = glowAttach
    local attach2Upper = Instance.new("Attachment")
    attach2Upper.Name = "2"
    attach2Upper.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 2.125, 0)
    attach2Upper.Parent = container
    local attach2Lower = Instance.new("Attachment")
    attach2Lower.Name = "2"
    attach2Lower.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
    attach2Lower.Parent = container
    local beamSafe1 = Instance.new("Beam")
    beamSafe1.Attachment0 = attach2Lower
    beamSafe1.Attachment1 = attach2Upper
    beamSafe1.Brightness = 1
    beamSafe1.Color = ColorSequence.new(AuraColors.Safe)
    beamSafe1.CurveSize0 = 0
    beamSafe1.CurveSize1 = 0
    beamSafe1.Enabled = true
    beamSafe1.FaceCamera = true
    beamSafe1.LightEmission = 1
    beamSafe1.LightInfluence = 1
    beamSafe1.Segments = 10
    beamSafe1.Texture = "rbxassetid://7673945506"
    beamSafe1.TextureLength = 0.3
    beamSafe1.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch
    beamSafe1.TextureSpeed = 2
    beamSafe1.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    beamSafe1.Width0 = 6
    beamSafe1.Width1 = 6
    beamSafe1.ZOffset = 1
    beamSafe1.Parent = container
    local beamSafe2 = Instance.new("Beam")
    beamSafe2.Attachment0 = attach2Lower
    beamSafe2.Attachment1 = attach2Upper
    beamSafe2.Brightness = 1
    beamSafe2.Color = ColorSequence.new(AuraColors.Safe)
    beamSafe2.CurveSize0 = 0
    beamSafe2.CurveSize1 = 0
    beamSafe2.Enabled = true
    beamSafe2.FaceCamera = true
    beamSafe2.LightEmission = 1
    beamSafe2.LightInfluence = 1
    beamSafe2.Segments = 10
    beamSafe2.Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=6045867277"
    beamSafe2.TextureLength = 0.1
    beamSafe2.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch
    beamSafe2.TextureSpeed = 1
    beamSafe2.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})

    beamSafe3.Segments = 10
    beamSafe3.Texture = "rbxassetid://1849531275"
    beamSafe3.TextureLength = 0.1
    beamSafe3.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch
    beamSafe3.TextureSpeed = 1
    beamSafe3.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    beamSafe3.Width0 = 6
    beamSafe3.Width1 = 6
    beamSafe3.ZOffset = 1
    beamSafe3.Parent = container
    local topSymbolAttach = Instance.new("Attachment")
    topSymbolAttach.Name = "1TopSymbol"
    topSymbolAttach.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 5.75001049, 1) * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.zero, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    topSymbolAttach.Parent = container
    local bottomSymbolAttach = Instance.new("Attachment")
    bottomSymbolAttach.Name = "1BottomSymbol"
    bottomSymbolAttach.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -1.24998808, 1) * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.zero, Vector3.new(0, 0, -1), Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0))
    bottomSymbolAttach.Parent = container
    local beamSymbol = Instance.new("Beam")
    beamSymbol.Attachment0 = topSymbolAttach
    beamSymbol.Attachment1 = bottomSymbolAttach
    beamSymbol.Brightness = 1
    beamSymbol.Color = ColorSequence.new(AuraColors.Safe)
    beamSymbol.CurveSize0 = 0.5
    beamSymbol.CurveSize1 = 0.5
    beamSymbol.Enabled = true
    beamSymbol.FaceCamera = false
    beamSymbol.LightEmission = 1
    beamSymbol.LightInfluence = 1
    beamSymbol.Segments = 100
    beamSymbol.Texture = "rbxassetid://8285797183"
    beamSymbol.TextureLength = 1
    beamSymbol.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Stretch
    beamSymbol.TextureSpeed = 0
    beamSymbol.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 0)
    beamSymbol.Width0 = 7
    beamSymbol.Width1 = 7
    beamSymbol.ZOffset = 0
    beamSymbol.Parent = container
end
local ToggledAuras = {}  
local function SetupAura(character, auraName)
    if not character then return end
    local AuraColor = AuraColors[auraName]  
    if auraName == "Safe" then
        AttachAuraSafe(character)
    end
end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
    for auraName, toggled in pairs(ToggledAuras) do
        if toggled then
            task.wait(0.5)  
            SetupAura(newChar, auraName)
        end
    end
end)
end
do
local lastStompTime = 0
local STOMP_COOLDOWN = 2
local STOMP_EFFECT = "Thanos"
do 
    local function boobs(targetCharacter)
        STOMP_EFFECT = States.StompEffect or "Spirit"
        local currentTime = tick()
        if currentTime - lastStompTime < STOMP_COOLDOWN then 
            return 
        end
        lastStompTime = currentTime
        if STOMP_EFFECT == "RoadRoller" then 
            RoadRoller.Parent = workspace
            RoadRoller.Anchored = true 
            RoadRoller.CFrame = targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 40, 0)
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://4877348549"
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
            Sound:Destroy()    
            TweenService:Create(
                RoadRoller,
                TweenInfo.new(0.2), 
                { 
                    CFrame = targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * 
                            CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) * 
                            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(200), -math.rad(200), math.rad(90)) 
                }
            ):Play()
            ShakeModule.StartShake(7, 0.5)
            task.wait(2)
            RoadRoller.CFrame = CFrame.new(20,2000,20)
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Rings" then 
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://7456436580"
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
            Sound:Destroy()
            local FakePart = Instance.new("Part",workspace)
            FakePart.Transparency = 1
            FakePart.Position = targetCharacter.Head.Position
            FakePart.Anchored = true 
            FakePart.CanCollide = false 
            SonicRings.Parent = FakePart
            task.wait(0.5)
            SonicRings.Parent = workspace
            FakePart:Destroy()
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "BlackHole" then 
            BlackHole.Parent = workspace
            BlackHole.Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position + Vector3.new(0,1,0)
            BlackHole.open:Play()
            ShakeModule.StartShake(7, 0.5)
            for _, emitter in pairs(BlackHole:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or emitter:IsA("Beam") then 
                    emitter.Enabled = true  
                end 
            end 
            task.wait(1.5)
            task.spawn(function()
                BlackHole.wind:Play()
                BlackHole.bring:Play()
                for _, part in pairs(targetCharacter:GetChildren()) do 
                    if not part:IsA("BasePart") then  end 
                    TweenService:Create(part,TweenInfo.new(1.4),{
                        Position = BlackHole.Event.WorldPosition
                    }):Play()
                end 
                task.wait(1.5)
                targetCharacter:Destroy()
            end)
            task.wait(3)
            TweenService:Create(BlackHole.wind,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                Volume = 0
            }):Play()
            for _, emitter in pairs(BlackHole:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or emitter:IsA("Beam") then 
                    emitter.Enabled = false 
                end 
            end 
            task.wait(2)
            BlackHole.Parent = Services.ReplicatedStorage
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Spirit" then 
            Spirit.Stomp:Play()
            Aura.Transparency = 0
            Aura.Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position 
            ShakeModule.StartShake(7, 0.5)
            TweenService:Create(Aura,TweenInfo.new(2),{
                Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position + Vector3.new(0,50,0),
                Size = Vector3.new(120, 90, 120),
                Transparency = 1
            }):Play()
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://6290067239"
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
            Sound:Destroy()
            for _, asset in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do 
                if not asset:IsA("BasePart") or not asset:IsA("Decal") then  end 
                TweenService:Create(asset,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                    Transparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end 
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Charm" then 
            local Stomp = Charm.FX.Stomp 
            local Sound = Charm.SFX:FindFirstChild("Explosive Hit 5")
            local TargetPos = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position
            local Emitters = {}
            local PartOne = Stomp.PartOne:Clone()
            local Part    = Stomp.Part:Clone()
            Charm.Parent = workspace
            PartOne.Position = TargetPos
            PartOne.Anchored = true 
            PartOne.Parent = workspace
            Part.Position = TargetPos
            Part.Anchored = true 
            Part.Parent = workspace
            for _, emitter in pairs(PartOne:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                    table.insert(Emitters,emitter)
                end 
            end 
            for _, emitter in pairs(Part:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                    table.insert(Emitters,emitter)
                end 
            end
            for _, emitter in pairs(Emitters) do 
                emitter.Enabled = true   
            end 
            local old_color = LightingService.FogColor
            Sound:Play()
            for _, asset in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do 
                if not asset:IsA("BasePart") or not asset:IsA("Decal") then  end 
                TweenService:Create(asset,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                    Transparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end 
            TweenService:Create(LightingService,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 42, 191)
            }):Play()
            task.wait(0.8)
            Part:Destroy()
            PartOne:Destroy()
            TweenService:Create(LightingService,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                FogColor = old_color
            }):Play()
            Charm.Parent = Services.ReplicatedStorage
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Thanos" then
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://3050376525"
            Sound:Play()
            for _, part in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do 
                if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("MeshPart") then 
                    local Thanos_Effect = Thanos:Clone() 
                    Thanos_Effect.Parent = part 
                    Thanos_Effect.Color =  ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, part.Color), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, Color3.fromRGB(65, 33, 18)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)) })
                    TweenService:Create(part,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                        Transparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                    task.delay(0.5,function()
                        Thanos_Effect.Enabled = false 
                    end)
                    task.wait(.1)
                end 
            end
            repeat task.wait() until Sound.TimeLength == 0
            Sound:Destroy()
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Afterslash" then 
            local AfterSlashEfx = AfterSlash:Clone()
            local UpperTorso = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            UpperTorso.Position = (CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position) * CFrame.new(0,35,0)).Position
            local Part = AfterSlashEfx.Part
            Part.Position = UpperTorso.Position
            Part.Parent = UpperTorso
            Part.Size = Vector3.new(5,7,5)
            for _ = 1, 25 do 
                task.wait(math.random(1,9) / 100)
                local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",Part)
                Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://5989945551"
                local DistortionSoundEffect = Instance.new("DistortionSoundEffect",Sound)
                Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
                Sound:Destroy()
                Part.Position = UpperTorso.Position
            end
            UpperTorso.Position = (CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position) * CFrame.new(0, -5, 0)).Position
            for _, emitter in pairs(Part:GetDescendants()) do
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                    emitter.Enabled = false
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
            local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment",workspace.Terrain)
            Attachment.WorldPosition = UpperTorso.Position
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",Part)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://4471648128" 
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true
            Sound:Destroy()
            for _, emitter in pairs(AfterSlashEfx.Burst:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                    emitter.Parent = Attachment
                    emitter:Emit(emitter:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
                end 
            end 
            for _, part in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    TweenService:Create(part, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {
                        Transparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                end
            end 
        end 
    end 
    function Modules:StompEffects(state)
        if not state then 
            DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("StompEffect")
            return 
        end 
        DhLibrary:AddConnection("StompEffect", RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local playerCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not playerCharacter or not playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or not playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") then
                return
            end
            local rayOrigin = playerCharacter.LowerTorso.Position
            local rayDirection = Vector3.new(0, -playerCharacter.UpperTorso.Size.y * 4.5, 0)
            local whitelist = {}
            for _, player in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= Services.LocalPlayer and player.Character and not  whitelist[player.Character] then
                    table.insert(whitelist, player.Character)
                end
            end
            if #whitelist > 0 then
                local hitPart, hitPosition, hitNormal = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithWhitelist(
                    Ray.new(rayOrigin, rayDirection),
                    whitelist,
                    false,
                    true
                )
                if hitPart then
                    local stompedCharacter = hitPart:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    if stompedCharacter and stompedCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and stompedCharacter:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") and stompedCharacter.BodyEffects.SDeath.Value and DhLibrary:IsAnimPlaying(playerCharacter,2816431506) then
                        boobs(stompedCharacter)
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
    end 
end 
do 
    function Modules:ImpactFrame()
        local Black = DhLibrary:Create("ColorCorrectionEffect",{
            TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),
        })
        local White = DhLibrary:Create("Highlight",{
            FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
            FillTransparency = 0
        })
        task.wait(.1)
        Black.Parent = LightingService
        White.Parent = Modules:GetCharacter("")
        task.wait(.1)
        White.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        Black.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        Black.Brightness = 1
        task.wait(.1)
        White:Destroy()
        Black:Destroy()
    end 
    function Modules:CloneChar(Character)
        local LocalChar = Modules:GetChar("")
        LocalChar.Archivable = true 
        local ClonedChar = LocalChar:Clone() 
        ClonedChar.Parent = Services.Workspace
        ClonedChar.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
        if ClonedChar:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then 
            ClonedChar:FindFirstChild("ForceField"):Destroy()
        end 
        local Player = Services.Players:FindFirstChild(Character.Name)
        if not Player then 
            return 
        end 
        local HumanoidDescription = Services.Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(Player.UserId)
        ClonedChar.Humanoid:ApplyDescription(HumanoidDescription)
        LocalChar.Archivable = false
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.BodyProportionScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.BodyProportionScale.Value
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.HeadScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.HeadScale.Value
        for _, accessory in pairs(ClonedChar:GetDescendants()) do 
            if accessory:IsA("Accessory") or accessory:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or accessory:IsA("Shirt") or accessory:IsA("Pants") then
                accessory:Destroy()
            end
            if accessory:IsA("BasePart") then 
                accessory.Transparency = 1
            end 
        end
        return ClonedChar 
    end 
    function Modules:AlignControl(part, part1, Offset)
        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        part.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        part.CFrame = part1.CFrame * (Offset or CFrame.new())
        part.CanCollide = false
        part1.CanCollide = false
    end
end 
end
do
local Visuals = {}
local ESPObjects = {}
local function createESP(plr)
    if plr == LocalPlayer then return end
    local esp = {
        Player = plr,
        Box = Drawing.new("Square"),
        BoxOutline = Drawing.new("Square"),
        Name = Drawing.new("Text"),
        Distance = Drawing.new("Text"),
        HealthBar = Drawing.new("Square"),
        HealthBarOutline = Drawing.new("Square"),
        HealthText = Drawing.new("Text"),
        Tracer = Drawing.new("Line"),
        TracerOutline = Drawing.new("Line"),
        HeadDot = Drawing.new("Circle"),
        LookVector = Drawing.new("Line"),
        Skeleton = {
            HeadToTorso = Drawing.new("Line"),
            TorsoToLeftArm = Drawing.new("Line"),
            TorsoToRightArm = Drawing.new("Line"),
            TorsoToLeftLeg = Drawing.new("Line"),
            TorsoToRightLeg = Drawing.new("Line")
        },
        Chams = Instance.new("Highlight")
    }
    esp.Box.Thickness = 1
    esp.Box.Filled = false
    esp.Box.Visible = false
    esp.Box.ZIndex = 2
    esp.BoxOutline.Thickness = 3
    esp.BoxOutline.Filled = false
    esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
    esp.BoxOutline.ZIndex = 1
    esp.BoxOutline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    esp.Name.Size = 13
    esp.Name.Center = true
    esp.Name.Outline = true
    esp.Name.Visible = false
    esp.Name.ZIndex = 2
    esp.Name.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    esp.Distance.Size = 11
    esp.Distance.Center = true
    esp.Distance.Outline = true
    esp.Distance.Visible = false
    esp.Distance.ZIndex = 2
    esp.Distance.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    esp.HealthBar.Thickness = 1
    esp.HealthBar.Filled = true
    esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
    esp.HealthBar.ZIndex = 2
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Thickness = 1
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Filled = true
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
    esp.HealthBarOutline.ZIndex = 1
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    esp.HealthText.Size = 10
    esp.HealthText.Center = false
    esp.HealthText.Outline = true
    esp.HealthText.Visible = false
    esp.HealthText.ZIndex = 2
    esp.HealthText.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    esp.Tracer.Thickness = 1.5
    esp.Tracer.Visible = false
    esp.Tracer.ZIndex = 2
    esp.TracerOutline.Thickness = 3
    esp.TracerOutline.Visible = false
    esp.TracerOutline.ZIndex = 1
    esp.TracerOutline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    esp.HeadDot.Radius = 3
    esp.HeadDot.Filled = true
    esp.HeadDot.Visible = false
    esp.HeadDot.ZIndex = 2
    esp.LookVector.Thickness = 1.5
    esp.LookVector.Visible = false
    esp.LookVector.ZIndex = 2
    for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do
        bone.Thickness = 1.5
        bone.Visible = false
        bone.ZIndex = 2
        bone.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end
    pcall(function()
        esp.Chams.Name = "SoulTech_Chams"
        esp.Chams.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 255)
        esp.Chams.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        esp.Chams.FillTransparency = 0.5
        esp.Chams.OutlineTransparency = 0
        esp.Chams.Parent = CoreGui
    end)
    ESPObjects[plr] = esp
end
local function removeESP(plr)
    local esp = ESPObjects[plr]
    if esp then
        pcall(function() esp.Box:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.BoxOutline:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Name:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Distance:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HealthBar:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HealthBarOutline:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HealthText:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Tracer:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.TracerOutline:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HeadDot:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.LookVector:Remove() end)
        for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do
            pcall(function() bone:Remove() end)
        end
        pcall(function() esp.Chams:Destroy() end)
        ESPObjects[plr] = nil
    end
end
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
        createESP(plr)
    end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(createESP)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeESP)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    for plr, esp in pairs(ESPObjects) do
        local char = plr.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if char and hum and hrp and head and hum.Health > 0 then
            local dist = (Camera.CFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if dist <= SoulSettings.ESP.MaxDistance then
                local hrpPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
                local headPos = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.6, 0))
                local legPos = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                if onScreen then
                    local boxHeight = math.abs(headPos.Y - legPos.Y)
                    local boxWidth = boxHeight * 0.65
                    local boxTopLeft = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X - boxWidth / 2, headPos.Y)
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.Box then
                        esp.Box.Size = Vector2.new(boxWidth, boxHeight)
                        esp.Box.Position = boxTopLeft
                        esp.Box.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.BoxColor
                        esp.Box.Visible = true
                        if SoulSettings.ESP.BoxOutline then
                            esp.BoxOutline.Size = Vector2.new(boxWidth, boxHeight)
                            esp.BoxOutline.Position = boxTopLeft
                            esp.BoxOutline.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.BoxOutlineColor
                            esp.BoxOutline.Visible = true
                        else
                            esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                        end
                    else
                        esp.Box.Visible = false
                        esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.Name then
                        esp.Name.Text = plr.DisplayName .. " (@" .. plr.Name .. ")"
                        esp.Name.Position = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, headPos.Y - 16)
                        esp.Name.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.Name.Visible = false
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.Distance then
                        esp.Distance.Text = string.format("[%d studs]", math.floor(dist))
                        esp.Distance.Position = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, legPos.Y + 2)
                        esp.Distance.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.Distance.Visible = false
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.HealthBar then
                        local hpPercent = math.clamp(hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
                        local barHeight = boxHeight * hpPercent
                        local barX = boxTopLeft.X - 6
                        esp.HealthBarOutline.Size = Vector2.new(4, boxHeight + 2)
                        esp.HealthBarOutline.Position = Vector2.new(barX - 1, boxTopLeft.Y - 1)
                        esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = true
                        esp.HealthBar.Size = Vector2.new(2, barHeight)
                        esp.HealthBar.Position = Vector2.new(barX, boxTopLeft.Y + (boxHeight - barHeight))
                        esp.HealthBar.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hpPercent * 0.33, 0.9, 1)
                        esp.HealthBar.Visible = true
                        if SoulSettings.ESP.HealthText then
                            esp.HealthText.Text = tostring(math.floor(hum.Health))
                            esp.HealthText.Position = Vector2.new(barX - 22, boxTopLeft.Y + (boxHeight - barHeight) - 2)
                            esp.HealthText.Visible = true
                        else
                            esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                        end
                    else
                        esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
                        esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
                        esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.Tracer then
                        local origin = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y)
                        if SoulSettings.ESP.TracerOrigin == "Center" then
                            origin = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
                        elseif SoulSettings.ESP.TracerOrigin == "Top" then
                            origin = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, 0)
                        elseif SoulSettings.ESP.TracerOrigin == "Mouse" then
                            origin = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                        end
                        esp.Tracer.From = origin
                        esp.Tracer.To = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, legPos.Y)
                        esp.Tracer.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.TracerColor
                        esp.Tracer.Visible = true
                        esp.TracerOutline.From = origin
                        esp.TracerOutline.To = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, legPos.Y)
                        esp.TracerOutline.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.Tracer.Visible = false
                        esp.TracerOutline.Visible = false
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.Chams then
                        esp.Chams.Adornee = char
                        esp.Chams.FillColor = SoulSettings.ESP.ChamsColor
                        esp.Chams.OutlineColor = SoulSettings.ESP.ChamsOutlineColor
                        esp.Chams.FillTransparency = SoulSettings.ESP.ChamFillTransparency
                        esp.Chams.Enabled = true
                    else
                        esp.Chams.Enabled = false
                    end
                else
                    esp.Box.Visible = false
                    esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                    esp.Name.Visible = false
                    esp.Distance.Visible = false
                    esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
                    esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
                    esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                    esp.Tracer.Visible = false
                    esp.TracerOutline.Visible = false
                    esp.Chams.Enabled = false
                end
            else
                esp.Box.Visible = false
                esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                esp.Name.Visible = false
                esp.Distance.Visible = false
                esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
                esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
                esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                esp.Tracer.Visible = false
                esp.TracerOutline.Visible = false
                esp.Chams.Enabled = false
            end
        else
            esp.Box.Visible = false
            esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
            esp.Name.Visible = false
            esp.Distance.Visible = false
            esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
            esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
            esp.HealthText.Visible = false
            esp.Tracer.Visible = false
            esp.TracerOutline.Visible = false
            if esp.Chams then esp.Chams.Enabled = false end
        end
    end
end)
local chinaHatMesh = nil
local chinaHatLight = nil
local function setupChinaHat()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local head = char:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
    if not head then return end
    if chinaHatMesh then chinaHatMesh:Destroy() end
    chinaHatMesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
    chinaHatMesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://1033714"
    chinaHatMesh.TextureId = ""
    chinaHatMesh.Scale = Vector3.new(SoulSettings.ChinaHat.ScaleX, SoulSettings.ChinaHat.ScaleY, SoulSettings.ChinaHat.ScaleZ)
    local hatPart = Instance.new("Part")
    hatPart.Name = "SoulTech_ChinaHat"
    hatPart.CanCollide = false
    hatPart.Anchored = false
    hatPart.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    hatPart.Color = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.Color
    hatPart.Transparency = 0.3
    hatPart.Size = Vector3.new(1, 0.2, 1)
    chinaHatMesh.Parent = hatPart
    chinaHatLight = Instance.new("PointLight")
    chinaHatLight.Range = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.LightRange
    chinaHatLight.Brightness = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.LightBrightness
    chinaHatLight.Color = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.LightColor
    chinaHatLight.Parent = hatPart
    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    weld.Part0 = head
    weld.Part1 = hatPart
    weld.Parent = hatPart
    hatPart.CFrame = head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, SoulSettings.ChinaHat.HoverOffset, 0)
    hatPart.Parent = char
end
local crosshairLines = {
    Top = Drawing.new("Line"),
    Bottom = Drawing.new("Line"),
    Left = Drawing.new("Line"),
    Right = Drawing.new("Line")
}
local crosshairText = Drawing.new("Text")
crosshairText.Size = 12
crosshairText.Center = true
crosshairText.Outline = true
crosshairText.Text = "Soul.Tech"
crosshairText.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 180, 255)
for _, line in pairs(crosshairLines) do
    line.Thickness = 1.5
    line.Visible = false
    line.ZIndex = 3
end
local crosshairAngle = 0
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if SoulSettings.Crosshair.Enabled then
        local center = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        if SoulSettings.Crosshair.FollowTarget and TargetPlr and TargetPlr.Character then
            local root = TargetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then
                local sp, os = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
                if os then center = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) end
            end
        end
        crosshairAngle = crosshairAngle + math.rad(SoulSettings.Crosshair.SpinSpeed) * dt * 60
        local gap = SoulSettings.Crosshair.Gap
        local len = SoulSettings.Crosshair.LineLength
        local col = SoulSettings.Crosshair.Color
        if SoulSettings.Crosshair.Rainbow then
            col = Color3.fromHSV((tick() * 0.5) % 1, 0.8, 1)
        end
        local angles = {
            Top = crosshairAngle,
            Right = crosshairAngle + math.pi / 2,
            Bottom = crosshairAngle + math.pi,
            Left = crosshairAngle + math.pi * 1.5
        }
        for name, line in pairs(crosshairLines) do
            local theta = angles[name]
            local dir = Vector2.new(math.cos(theta), math.sin(theta))
            line.From = center + dir * gap
            line.To = center + dir * (gap + len)
            line.Color = col
            line.Thickness = SoulSettings.Crosshair.Thickness
            line.Visible = true
        end
        if SoulSettings.Crosshair.ShowText then
            crosshairText.Position = center + Vector2.new(0, gap + len + 4)
            crosshairText.Color = col
            crosshairText.Visible = true
        else
            crosshairText.Visible = false
        end
    else
        for _, line in pairs(crosshairLines) do line.Visible = false end
        crosshairText.Visible = false
    end
end)
local rainEmitter = nil
local rainPart = nil
local function setupRain()
    if rainPart then rainPart:Destroy() end
    if not SoulSettings.Rain.Enabled then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if not hrp then return end
    rainPart = Instance.new("Part")
    rainPart.Name = "SoulTech_RainPart"
    rainPart.Transparency = 1
    rainPart.CanCollide = false
    rainPart.Anchored = false
    rainPart.Size = Vector3.new(100, 1, 100)
    rainPart.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0)
    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
    weld.Part0 = hrp
    weld.Part1 = rainPart
    weld.Parent = rainPart
    rainPart.Parent = char
    rainEmitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    rainEmitter.Rate = SoulSettings.Rain.Rate
    rainEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(SoulSettings.Rain.Speed, SoulSettings.Rain.Speed * 1.2)
    rainEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(SoulSettings.Rain.Lifetime)
    rainEmitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.08, 0.12)
    rainEmitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.Rain.Color)
    rainEmitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.2, 0.8)
    rainEmitter.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
    rainEmitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(5, 5)
    rainEmitter.Parent = rainPart
end
end
do
local function NewRadarCircle(Transparency, Color, Radius, Filled, Thickness)
    local c = Drawing.new("Circle")
    c.Transparency = Transparency
    c.Color = Color
    c.Visible = false
    c.Thickness = Thickness
    c.Position = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    c.Radius = Radius
    c.NumSides = math.clamp(Radius * 55 / 100, 10, 75)
    c.Filled = Filled
    return c
end
local RadarBackground = NewRadarCircle(0.9, RadarInfo.RadarBack, RadarInfo.Radius, true, 1)
local RadarBorder = NewRadarCircle(0.75, RadarInfo.RadarBorder, RadarInfo.Radius, false, 3)
local function GetRadarRelative(pos)
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char and char.PrimaryPart then
        local pmpart = char.PrimaryPart
        local camerapos = NewVector3(Camera.CFrame.Position.X, pmpart.Position.Y, Camera.CFrame.Position.Z)
        local newcf = NewCFrame(pmpart.Position, camerapos)
        local r = newcf:PointToObjectSpace(pos)
        return r.X, r.Z
    end
    return 0, 0
end
local RadarDots = {}
local function CreateRadarDot(plr)
    local dot = NewRadarCircle(1, RadarInfo.PlayerDot, 3, true, 1)
    RadarDots[plr] = dot
end
local function RemoveRadarDot(plr)
    if RadarDots[plr] then
        RadarDots[plr]:Remove()
        RadarDots[plr] = nil
    end
end
for _, v in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if v ~= LocalPlayer then
        CreateRadarDot(v)
    end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(v)
    if v ~= LocalPlayer then
        CreateRadarDot(v)
    end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(RemoveRadarDot)
local function NewLocalRadarDot()
    local d = Drawing.new("Triangle")
    d.Visible = false
    d.Thickness = 1
    d.Filled = true
    d.Color = RadarInfo.LocalPlayerDot
    return d
end
local LocalRadarDot = NewLocalRadarDot()
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    local visible = RadarInfo.Enabled
    RadarBackground.Visible = visible
    RadarBorder.Visible = visible
    RadarBackground.Position = RadarInfo.Position
    RadarBackground.Radius = RadarInfo.Radius
    RadarBackground.Color = RadarInfo.RadarBack
    RadarBorder.Position = RadarInfo.Position
    RadarBorder.Radius = RadarInfo.Radius
    RadarBorder.Color = RadarInfo.RadarBorder
    if LocalRadarDot then
        LocalRadarDot.Visible = visible
        LocalRadarDot.Color = RadarInfo.LocalPlayerDot
        LocalRadarDot.PointA = RadarInfo.Position + Vector2.new(0, -6)
        LocalRadarDot.PointB = RadarInfo.Position + Vector2.new(-3, 6)
        LocalRadarDot.PointC = RadarInfo.Position + Vector2.new(3, 6)
    end
    if not visible then
        for _, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do
            dot.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end
    for plr, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do
        local char = plr.Character
        if char and char.PrimaryPart then
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local relx, rely = GetRadarRelative(char.PrimaryPart.Position)
                local newpos = RadarInfo.Position - Vector2.new(relx * RadarInfo.Scale, rely * RadarInfo.Scale)
                if (newpos - RadarInfo.Position).Magnitude < RadarInfo.Radius - 2 then
                    dot.Radius = 3
                    dot.Position = newpos
                else
                    local dist = (RadarInfo.Position - newpos).Magnitude
                    local calc = (RadarInfo.Position - newpos).Unit * (dist - RadarInfo.Radius)
                    dot.Radius = 2
                    dot.Position = Vector2.new(newpos.X + calc.X, newpos.Y + calc.Y)
                end
                dot.Visible = true
                dot.Color = RadarInfo.PlayerDot
                if RadarInfo.Team_Check then
                    dot.Color = (plr.TeamColor == LocalPlayer.TeamColor) and RadarInfo.Team or RadarInfo.Enemy
                end
                if RadarInfo.Health_Color then
                    dot.Color = HealthBarLerp(hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth)
                end
            else
                dot.Visible = false
            end
        else
            dot.Visible = false
        end
    end
end)
local RadarInset = game:GetService("GuiService"):GetGuiInset()
local RadarDragging = false
local RadarOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and RadarInfo.Enabled then
        if (Vector2.new(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y + RadarInset.Y) - RadarInfo.Position).Magnitude < RadarInfo.Radius then
            RadarOffset = RadarInfo.Position - Vector2.new(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y)
            RadarDragging = true
        end
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        RadarDragging = false
    end
end)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if RadarDragging then
        RadarInfo.Position = Vector2.new(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y) + RadarOffset
    end
end)
    local ProtectGui = protectgui or (syn and syn.protect_gui) or (function() end);
    local ScreenGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui');
    ProtectGui(ScreenGui);
    ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global;
    ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui;
    local Toggles = {};
    local Options = {};
    getgenv().Toggles = Toggles;
    getgenv().Options = Options;
    local Library = {
        Registry = {};
        RegistryMap = {};
        HudRegistry = {};
        FontColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255);
        MainColor = Color3.fromHex("#232330");
        BackgroundColor = Color3.fromHex("#1d1b26");
        AccentColor = Color3.fromRGB(173, 216, 230);
        OutlineColor = Color3.fromHex("#27232f");
        RiskColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50),
        Black = Color3.new(0, 0, 0);
        Font = Enum.Font.Code,
        OpenedFrames = {};
        DependencyBoxes = {};
        Signals = {};
        ScreenGui = ScreenGui;
    };
    local RainbowStep = 0
    local Hue = 0
    table.insert(Library.Signals, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(Delta)
        RainbowStep = RainbowStep + Delta
        if RainbowStep >= (1 / 60) then
            RainbowStep = 0
            Hue = Hue + (1 / 400);
            if Hue > 1 then
                Hue = 0;
            end;
            Library.CurrentRainbowHue = Hue;
            Library.CurrentRainbowColor = Color3.fromHSV(Hue, 0.8, 1);
        end
    end))
    local function GetPlayersString()
        local PlayerList = Players:GetPlayers();
        for i = 1, #PlayerList do
            PlayerList[i] = PlayerList[i].Name;
        end;
        table.sort(PlayerList, function(str1, str2) return str1 < str2 end);
        return PlayerList;
    end;
    local function GetTeamsString()
        local TeamList = game:GetService('Teams'):GetTeams();
        for i = 1, #TeamList do
            TeamList[i] = TeamList[i].Name;
        end;
        table.sort(TeamList, function(str1, str2) return str1 < str2 end);
        return TeamList;
    end;
end
do
function RunPredictAutoArmor(dt)
		local target = (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target)
			or (Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled and Settings.Misc.Players.Target)
			or nil
		if not target or not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
		local lhrp = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not lhrp then return end
		if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then return end
		local char = target.Character
		local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp or (hrp.Position - lhrp.Position).Magnitude <= 25.1 then return end
		local rpg = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("[RPG]")
		if not rpg then return end
		local lastPos       = hrp.Position
		local isInPosition  = nil
		local shopArmorData = workspace:FindFirstChild("Shops")
		if shopArmorData then
			for _, shopModel in ipairs(shopArmorData:GetChildren()) do
				local button = shopModel:FindFirstChild("Button") or shopModel:FindFirstChild("button")
				if button then
					if (lastPos - button.Position).Magnitude <= 9 then
						isInPosition = button.Position
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local targetBodyEffects = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
		local targetFF = targetBodyEffects and targetBodyEffects:FindFirstChild("ForceField") and targetBodyEffects.ForceField.Value
		if targetFF then
			isInPosition = nil
		end
		local ignored = workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
		local has_one = false
		if ignored then
			for _, instance in ipairs(ignored:GetChildren()) do
				if instance.Name == "Model" then
					local part = instance:FindFirstChild("Launcher")
					if part then
						local ok, owned = pcall(isnetworkowner, part)
						if ok and owned then
							has_one = true
						end
						local bv = part:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity")
						if bv then bv:Destroy() end
						local ti = part:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest")
						if ti then ti:Destroy() end
						if isInPosition then
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(isInPosition + NewVector3(0, math.random(-185, -150) / 100, 0))
						else
							local aimPos = (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition())
								or (hrp.Position)
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(aimPos + NewVector3(0, 100, 0))
						end
						part.Velocity = NewVector3(0, 1.85, 0)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if not has_one and PredictAutoArmorReady and not targetFF then
			PredictAutoArmorReady = false
			task.spawn(function()
				local oldTool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
				if oldTool then
					oldTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
					task.wait(2)
				end
				local rpgRef = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("[RPG]")
				if rpgRef then
					rpgRef.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
					task.wait(0.03)
					if rpgRef.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character then
						rpgRef:Activate()
					end
					task.delay(2.5, function()
						PredictAutoArmorReady = true
					end)
					task.wait(0.03)
					if MainEvent then
						MainEvent:FireServer("Reload", rpgRef)
					end
					if rpgRef and rpgRef.Parent then
						rpgRef.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
					end
					task.wait(0.03)
					if oldTool and oldTool.Parent == LocalPlayer.Backpack then
						oldTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
					end
				else
					PredictAutoArmorReady = true
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, function(dt)
		if Settings.Misc.Other.PredictAutoArmor.Enabled then
			pcall(RunPredictAutoArmor, dt)
		end
	end)
	local SilentAim = {} do
	    SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.SilentAim.Target, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
            local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Root" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Head" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.SilentAim.Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.SilentAim.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and InAir then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Velocity.X, Abs(Velocity.Y * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiGroundShots.ToTakeOff), Velocity.Z)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Humanization.Enabled then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Humanization.Value * 0.01)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.JumpOffset.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.SilentAim.Target) then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end
            return PredictedPosition
	    end
	    local RoundMultiple = function(...)
            local A = {}
            for I, V in next, table.pack(...) do
                A[I] = Round(V)
            end
            return unpack(A)
        end
        local WorldToViewport = function(...)
            local A, B = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(...)
            return NewVector2(A.X, A.Y), B, A.Z
        end
        SilentAim.BoxFov = function(Player)
            local Character = Player and Player.Character
            if Character then
                local CFrame = Character:GetModelCFrame()
                local Position, Visible, Depth = WorldToViewport(Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                Drawings.SilentAimBox.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                if Visible then
                    local ScaleFactor = 1 / (Depth * Tan(Rad(Camera.FieldOfView / 2)) * 2) * 1000
                    local Width, Height = RoundMultiple(4 * ScaleFactor * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.Maximum, 7 * ScaleFactor * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.Maximum)
                    local X, Y = RoundMultiple(Position.X, Position.Y)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size = NewVector2(Width, Height)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position = NewVector2(RoundMultiple(X - Width / 2, Y - Height / 2))
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Color = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Color
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Size = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Position = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Size = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size - NewVector2(2, 2)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Position = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position + NewVector2(1, 1)
                end
            else
                Drawings.SilentAimBox.Visible = false
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Visible = false
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Visible = false
            end
        end
	end
	local AimAssist = {} do
	    AimAssist.MouseMoveRel = function(Position, Smoothing)
		    local MousePosition = UserInputServive:GetMouseLocation()
	    	mousemoverel((Position.X - MousePosition.X) / Smoothing, (Position.Y - MousePosition.Y) / Smoothing)
	    end
	    AimAssist.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.AimAssist.Target, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
            local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Root" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Head" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.AimAssist.Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.AimAssist.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.AimAssist.Target) then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Velocity.X, Abs(Velocity.Y * Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AntiGroundShots.ToTakeOff), Velocity.Z)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Shake.Enabled then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Shake.Amount)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.JumpOffset.Enabled and InAir then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end
            return PredictedPosition
	    end
	end
	local HitDetection = {} do
        do
            do
                local Part = Instance.new('Part')
                Part.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
                local Attachment = Instance.new('Attachment')
                Attachment.Name = 'Attachment'
                Attachment.Parent = Part
                Table.HitEffect['Crescent Slash'] = Attachment
                local Glow = Instance.new('ParticleEmitter')
                Glow.Name = 'Glow'
                Glow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.16, 0.16)
                Glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1421725, 0.6182796), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
                Glow.Color = ColorSequence.new(NewRGB(91, 177, 252))
                Glow.Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
                Glow.Brightness = 5
                Glow.Size = NumberSequence.new(9.1873131, 16.5032349)
                Glow.Enabled = false
                Glow.ZOffset = -0.0565939
                Glow.Rate = 50
                Glow.Texture = 'rbxassetid://8708637750'
                local Gradient1 = Instance.new('ParticleEmitter')
                Gradient1.Name = 'Gradient1'
                Gradient1.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.3)
                Gradient1.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.15, 0.3), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
                Gradient1.Color = ColorSequence.new(NewRGB(115, 201, 255))
                Gradient1.Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
                Gradient1.Brightness = 6
                Gradient1.Size = NumberSequence.new(0, 11.6261358)
                Gradient1.Enabled = false
                Gradient1.ZOffset = 0.9187313
                Gradient1.Rate = 50
                Gradient1.Texture = 'rbxassetid://8196169974'
                Gradient1.Parent = Attachment
                local Shards = Instance.new('ParticleEmitter')
                Shards.Name = 'Shards'
                Shards.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.19, 0.7)
