        fovCircle.Visible = false
        fovFillCircle.Visible = false
    end
end)
local previousPositions = {}
local previousVelocities = {}
local calculatedVelocities = {}
local calculatedAccelerations = {}
local function updateVelocityCalculations()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local hrp = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local prev = previousPositions[plr.UserId]
            local now = tick()
            if prev then
                local dt = now - prev.Time
                if dt > 0.001 then
                    local calcVel = (hrp.Position - prev.Pos) / dt
                    calculatedVelocities[plr.UserId] = calcVel
                    local prevVel = previousVelocities[plr.UserId]
                    if prevVel then
                        local calcAcc = (calcVel - prevVel.Vel) / dt
                        calculatedAccelerations[plr.UserId] = calcAcc
                    end
                    previousVelocities[plr.UserId] = { Vel = calcVel, Time = now }
                end
            end
            previousPositions[plr.UserId] = { Pos = hrp.Position, Time = now }
        end
    end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(updateVelocityCalculations)
local function getResolvedVelocity(player, mode)
    if not player or not player.Character then return Vector3.zero end
    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return Vector3.zero end
    local rawVel = hrp.Velocity
    local calcVel = calculatedVelocities[player.UserId] or rawVel
    local calcAcc = calculatedAccelerations[player.UserId] or Vector3.zero
    if mode == "Recalculate" or mode == "Delta" then
        return calcVel
    elseif mode == "MoveDirection" then
        local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            return hum.MoveDirection * (hum.WalkSpeed or 16)
        end
        return rawVel
    elseif mode == "Angle Trig" then
        local hum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
            local dir = hum.MoveDirection
            local speed = hum.WalkSpeed or 16
            local theta = math.atan2(dir.Z, dir.X)
            return Vector3.new(math.cos(theta) * speed, rawVel.Y, math.sin(theta) * speed)
        end
        return rawVel
    elseif mode == "Acceleration" then
        return calcVel + (calcAcc * 0.05)
    elseif mode == "Zero" or mode == "Zero Velocity" then
        return Vector3.zero
    elseif mode == "Physics" then
        return hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity or rawVel
    elseif mode == "Invert" then
        return -rawVel
    elseif mode == "Desync Resolver" or mode == "Prediction Breaker" then
        if rawVel.Magnitude > 80 or rawVel.Magnitude < -80 or math.abs(rawVel.Y) > 50 then
            return calcVel
        end
        return rawVel
    else
        if rawVel.Magnitude > 80 then
            return calcVel
        end
        return rawVel
    end
end
local function calculateBallisticTrajectory(origin, targetPos, bulletSpeed, gravity)
    local diff = targetPos - origin
    local flatDist = Vector2.new(diff.X, diff.Z).Magnitude
    local timeToHit = flatDist / math.max(bulletSpeed, 100)
    local drop = 0.5 * gravity * (timeToHit ^ 2)
    return targetPos + Vector3.new(0, drop, 0)
end
local function getTargetAimPosition(target, settingsTable)
    if not target or not target.Character then return nil end
    local isAir = isPlayerInAir(target)
    local hitPart = getTargetHitPart(target, isAir, settingsTable)
    if not hitPart then return nil end
    local pred = settingsTable.Prediction
    if settingsTable.AutoPredict then
        pred = getPingPrediction()
    end
    local vel = getResolvedVelocity(target, settingsTable.ResolverMode)
    local pos = hitPart.Position + (vel * pred)
    if isAir and settingsTable.JumpOffset then
        pos = pos + Vector3.new(0, settingsTable.JumpOffset, 0)
    end
    if settingsTable.AntiGroundShots and not isAir then
        pos = pos + Vector3.new(0, settingsTable.AntiGroundOffset or 0.5, 0)
    end
    if settingsTable.Offset then
        pos = pos + settingsTable.Offset
    end
    return pos, hitPart
end
local function getMultiPointHitPart(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return nil end
    local char = player.Character
    local parts = {
        "Head", "UpperTorso", "HumanoidRootPart", "LowerTorso",
        "LeftUpperArm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg"
    }
    for _, name in ipairs(parts) do
        local p = char:FindFirstChild(name)
        if p and isPlayerVisible(p) then
            return p
        end
    end
    return char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local strafeAngle = 0
local strafeLine = Drawing.new("Line")
strafeLine.Thickness = 2
strafeLine.Visible = false
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if SoulSettings.Camlock.Enabled and TargetPlr and TargetPlr.Character then
        if SoulSettings.Camlock.UnlockOnDeath and not isPlayerAlive(TargetPlr) then
            TargetPlr = nil
            TargetPart = nil
            strafeLine.Visible = false
            return
        end
        if SoulSettings.Camlock.UnlockOnKO and isPlayerKnocked(TargetPlr) then
            TargetPlr = nil
            TargetPart = nil
            strafeLine.Visible = false
            return
        end
        local aimPos, hitPart = getTargetAimPosition(TargetPlr, SoulSettings.Camlock)
        if aimPos then
            if SoulSettings.Camlock.Smoothing then
                local currentCF = Camera.CFrame
                local targetCF = CFrame.new(currentCF.Position, aimPos)
                Camera.CFrame = currentCF:Lerp(targetCF, SoulSettings.Camlock.Smoothness)
            else
                Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(Camera.CFrame.Position, aimPos)
            end
            if SoulSettings.Camlock.LookAt and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local myHRP = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                myHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(myHRP.Position, Vector3.new(aimPos.X, myHRP.Position.Y, aimPos.Z))
            end
            if SoulSettings.Camlock.ToggleStrafe and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                strafeAngle = strafeAngle + 0.05
                local radius = 8
                local offsetX = math.cos(strafeAngle) * radius
                local offsetZ = math.sin(strafeAngle) * radius
                local targetHRP = TargetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if targetHRP then
                    local myHRP = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    local newPos = targetHRP.Position + Vector3.new(offsetX, 0, offsetZ)
                    if SoulSettings.Camlock.VoldBypass then
                        myHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(newPos, targetHRP.Position)
                    else
                        myHRP.Position = newPos
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.Camlock.VisualizeStrafe then
                        local p1, on1 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(myHRP.Position)
                        local p2, on2 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetHRP.Position)
                        if on1 and on2 then
                            strafeLine.From = Vector2.new(p1.X, p1.Y)
                            strafeLine.To = Vector2.new(p2.X, p2.Y)
                            strafeLine.Color = SoulSettings.Camlock.VisInline
                            strafeLine.Visible = true
                        else
                            strafeLine.Visible = false
                        end
                    else
                        strafeLine.Visible = false
                    end
                end
            else
                strafeLine.Visible = false
            end
        end
    else
        strafeLine.Visible = false
    end
end)
local function getSilentAimTarget()
    if not SoulSettings.SilentAim.Enabled then return nil end
    local target = getClosestPlayerToCursor(
        SoulSettings.SilentAim.FOV.Radius,
        SoulSettings.SilentAim.CheckWall,
        SoulSettings.SilentAim.CheckForcefield,
        SoulSettings.SilentAim.CheckTeam,
        SoulSettings.SilentAim.CheckAlive
    )
    if target and target.Character then
        local aimPos, part = getTargetAimPosition(target, SoulSettings.SilentAim)
        if math.random(1, 100) <= SoulSettings.SilentAim.HitChance then
            return target, aimPos, part
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local oldNamecall = nil
oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(self, ...)
    local method = getnamecallmethod()
    local args = { ... }
    if not checkcaller() and SoulSettings.SilentAim.Enabled then
        if method == "FireServer" or method == "fireServer" then
            if self.Name == "MainEvent" or self.Name == ".RemoteEvent" or self.Name == "Remote" then
                local firstArg = args[1]
                if firstArg == "UpdateMousePos" or firstArg == "UpdateMousePosI" or firstArg == "MousePos" or firstArg == "Bullets" then
                    local target, aimPos, part = getSilentAimTarget()
                    if aimPos then
                        args[2] = aimPos
                        return oldNamecall(self, unpack(args))
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if not checkcaller() and SoulSettings.GunMods.Wallbang then
        if method == "FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList" or method == "findPartOnRayWithIgnoreList" or method == "Raycast" or method == "raycast" then
            local target, aimPos, part = getSilentAimTarget()
            if part then
                return part, aimPos, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), part.Material
            end
        end
    end
    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
end))
local oldIndex = nil
oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(function(self, prop)
    if not checkcaller() and SoulSettings.SilentAim.Enabled and (prop == "Hit" or prop == "hit") and self:IsA("Mouse") then
        local target, aimPos, part = getSilentAimTarget()
        if aimPos then
            return CFrame.new(aimPos)
        end
    end
    if not checkcaller() and SoulSettings.SilentAim.Enabled and (prop == "Target" or prop == "target") and self:IsA("Mouse") then
        local target, aimPos, part = getSilentAimTarget()
        if part then
            return part
        end
    end
    return oldIndex(self, prop)
end))
local lastTriggerTime = 0
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not SoulSettings.TriggerBot.Enabled then return end
    if tick() - lastTriggerTime < SoulSettings.TriggerBot.Delay then return end
    local target = nil
    if SoulSettings.TriggerBot.OnlyTarget then
        target = TargetPlr
    else
        target = getClosestPlayerToCursor(
            SoulSettings.TriggerBot.FOV,
            SoulSettings.TriggerBot.CheckWall,
            SoulSettings.TriggerBot.CheckForcefield,
            SoulSettings.TriggerBot.CheckTeam,
            SoulSettings.TriggerBot.CheckAlive
        )
    end
    if target and target.Character then
        local isAir = isPlayerInAir(target)
        local part = getTargetHitPart(target, isAir, SoulSettings.SilentAim)
        if part then
            lastTriggerTime = tick()
            local tool = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if tool then
                tool:Activate()
            end
        end
    end
end)
local function updateHitboxExpander()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            if SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.TeamCheck and plr.Team == LocalPlayer.Team then  end
            local part = plr.Character:FindFirstChild(SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.HitPart) or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if part then
                if SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.Enabled then
                    part.Size = Vector3.new(SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.Size, SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.Size, SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.Size)
                    part.Transparency = SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.FillTransparency
                    part.CanCollide = false
                    if SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.Visualize then
                        part.Color = SoulSettings.HitboxExpander.Color
                        part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
                    end
                else
                    part.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                    part.Transparency = 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(updateHitboxExpander)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not SoulSettings.KillAura.Enabled then return end
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not myChar or not myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    local myHRP = myChar.HumanoidRootPart
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character and isPlayerAlive(plr) and not isPlayerKnocked(plr) then
            local enemyHRP = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if enemyHRP then
                local dist = (myHRP.Position - enemyHRP.Position).Magnitude
                if dist <= SoulSettings.KillAura.Range then
                    local tool = myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                    if tool then
                        tool:Activate()
                        if MainRemoteInstance then
                            MainRemoteInstance:FireServer("UpdateMousePos", enemyHRP.Position)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
end
do
local shakeActive = false
local shakeStartTime = 0
local shakeDuration = 0.8
local initialIntensity = 1
local ShakeModule = {}
function ShakeModule.StartShake(intensity, duration)
    initialIntensity = intensity or 1
    shakeDuration = duration or 0.8
    shakeStartTime = os.clock()
    shakeActive = true
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not shakeActive then return end
    local elapsed = os.clock() - shakeStartTime
    if elapsed >= shakeDuration then
        shakeActive = false
        return
    end
    local progress = elapsed / shakeDuration
    local currentIntensity = initialIntensity * (1 - progress^2)
    local timeFactor = os.clock() * 10
    local shakeX = math.noise(timeFactor, 0) * currentIntensity * 0.1
    local shakeY = math.noise(timeFactor, 100) * currentIntensity * 0.1
    local shakeZ = math.noise(timeFactor, 200) * currentIntensity * 0.1
    Camera.CFrame = Camera.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(shakeX, shakeY, shakeZ)
end)
local flying = false
local flySpeed = 2
local flyBodyVelocity = nil
local flyBodyGyro = nil
local function toggleFly(bool)
    flying = bool
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    local hrp = char.HumanoidRootPart
    if flying then
        flyBodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        flyBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
        flyBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        flyBodyVelocity.Parent = hrp
        flyBodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        flyBodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
        flyBodyGyro.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
        flyBodyGyro.Parent = hrp
    else
        if flyBodyVelocity then flyBodyVelocity:Destroy() flyBodyVelocity = nil end
        if flyBodyGyro then flyBodyGyro:Destroy() flyBodyGyro = nil end
    end
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if flying and flyBodyVelocity and flyBodyGyro and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local camCF = Camera.CFrame
        local moveDir = Vector3.zero
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then moveDir = moveDir + camCF.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then moveDir = moveDir - camCF.LookVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then moveDir = moveDir - camCF.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then moveDir = moveDir + camCF.RightVector end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then moveDir = moveDir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then moveDir = moveDir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
        flyBodyVelocity.Velocity = moveDir * (SoulSettings.Movement.FlySpeed * 25)
        flyBodyGyro.CFrame = camCF
    end
end)
function Modules:VehicleFly()
    if not States.AllowedVehicleFly then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or not hum.SeatPart then return end
    local seat = hum.SeatPart
    local bodyVel = seat:FindFirstChild("SoulTech_VehicleFly") or Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bodyVel.Name = "SoulTech_VehicleFly"
    bodyVel.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
    bodyVel.Parent = seat
    local bodyGyro = seat:FindFirstChild("SoulTech_VehicleGyro") or Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    bodyGyro.Name = "SoulTech_VehicleGyro"
    bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
    bodyGyro.Parent = seat
    local cam = Camera
    local speed = States.VehicleFlySpeed or 2
    local dir = Vector3.zero
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir = dir + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir = dir - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir = dir - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir = dir + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dir = dir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then dir = dir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
    bodyVel.Velocity = dir * (speed * 30)
    bodyGyro.CFrame = cam.CFrame
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if SoulSettings.Movement.WalkSpeedEnabled and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = SoulSettings.Movement.WalkSpeed end
    end
    if SoulSettings.Movement.JumpPowerEnabled and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            hum.JumpPower = SoulSettings.Movement.JumpPower
        end
    end
    if SoulSettings.Movement.BunnyHop and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
            hum.Jump = true
        end
    end
    if SoulSettings.Movement.SpinBot and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(SoulSettings.Movement.SpinSpeed), 0)
    end
    if SoulSettings.Movement.CFrameSpeed and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        if hum and hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then
            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame + (hum.MoveDirection * (SoulSettings.Movement.SpeedAmount * 0.2))
        end
    end
    if SoulSettings.Movement.AntiSlow and LocalPlayer.Character then
        local be = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
        if be then
            local slow = be:FindFirstChild("Movement") or be:FindFirstChild("Slow")
            if slow then
                for _, s in pairs(slow:GetChildren()) do
                    pcall(function() s:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)
local noclipConnection = nil
local function toggleNoclip(bool)
    SoulSettings.Movement.Noclip = bool
    if bool then
        noclipConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                for _, part in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        if noclipConnection then
            noclipConnection:Disconnect()
            noclipConnection = nil
        end
    end
end
local disabledSeats = {}
local function disableSeat(seat, bool)
    if seat and seat:IsA("Seat") then
        seat.Disabled = bool
    end
end
for _, seat in pairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
    if seat:IsA("Seat") then table.insert(disabledSeats, seat) end
end
Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    if obj:IsA("Seat") then
        table.insert(disabledSeats, obj)
        disableSeat(obj, SoulSettings.Movement.NoSeat)
    end
end)
local BodyCloneDesync = nil
pcall(function()
    BodyCloneDesync = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://8246626421")[1]
    BodyCloneDesync.Parent = Workspace
    if BodyCloneDesync:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then BodyCloneDesync.Humanoid:Destroy() end
    if BodyCloneDesync:FindFirstChild("Head") and BodyCloneDesync.Head:FindFirstChild("Face") then
        BodyCloneDesync.Head.Face:Destroy()
    end
    for _, v in pairs(BodyCloneDesync:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") then
            v.CanCollide = false
            v.Transparency = 0.5
        end
    end
    BodyCloneDesync.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(9999, 9999, 9999)
end)
local DesyncLine = Drawing.new("Line")
DesyncLine.Thickness = 2
DesyncLine.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
DesyncLine.Visible = false
local DesyncDot = Drawing.new("Circle")
DesyncDot.Radius = 6
DesyncDot.Filled = true
DesyncDot.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
DesyncDot.Visible = false
local DesyncStatus = Drawing.new("Text")
DesyncStatus.Text = "Desync: OFF"
DesyncStatus.Size = 15
DesyncStatus.Font = 2
DesyncStatus.Outline = true
DesyncStatus.Position = Vector2.new(120, 120)
DesyncStatus.Visible = false
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not LocalPlayer.Character or not LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        if BodyCloneDesync then BodyCloneDesync:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(9999, 9999, 9999)) end
        DesyncLine.Visible = false
        DesyncDot.Visible = false
        DesyncStatus.Visible = false
        return
    end
    local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
    local oldCF = hrp.CFrame
    local desyncCF = oldCF
    if SoulSettings.Desync.Enabled then
        local mode = SoulSettings.Desync.Mode
        if mode == "Destroy Cheaters" then
            desyncCF = CFrame.new(9e9, 1, 1) * oldCF.Rotation
        elseif mode == "Underground" then
            desyncCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 12, 0)) * oldCF.Rotation
        elseif mode == "Void Spam" then
            desyncCF = math.random(1, 2) == 1 and oldCF or CFrame.new(math.random(10000, 50000), math.random(10000, 50000), math.random(10000, 50000)) * oldCF.Rotation
        elseif mode == "Void" then
            desyncCF = oldCF + Vector3.new(math.random(-44444, 44444), math.random(-44444, 44444), math.random(-44444, 44444))
        elseif mode == "Random" then
            local amt = SoulSettings.Desync.RandomAmount
            desyncCF = CFrame.new(oldCF.Position + Vector3.new(math.random(-amt, amt), math.random(-amt/2, amt/2), math.random(-amt, amt))) * oldCF.Rotation
        elseif mode == "Safe Shoot" then
            desyncCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(math.random(0, 360), math.random(0, 360), math.rad(180))
        elseif mode == "Custom" then
            desyncCF = CFrame.new(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(SoulSettings.Desync.CustomX, SoulSettings.Desync.CustomY, SoulSettings.Desync.CustomZ)) * oldCF.Rotation
        end
        if SoulSettings.Desync.Visualize and BodyCloneDesync then
            BodyCloneDesync:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(desyncCF)
        end
        if SoulSettings.Desync.Line then
            local p1, on1 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(oldCF.Position)
            local p2, on2 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(desyncCF.Position)
            if on1 and on2 then
                DesyncLine.From = Vector2.new(p1.X, p1.Y)
                DesyncLine.To = Vector2.new(p2.X, p2.Y)
                DesyncLine.Visible = true
            else
                DesyncLine.Visible = false
            end
        else
            DesyncLine.Visible = false
        end
        if SoulSettings.Desync.Dot then
            local p2, on2 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(desyncCF.Position)
            if on2 then
                DesyncDot.Position = Vector2.new(p2.X, p2.Y)
                DesyncDot.Visible = true
            else
                DesyncDot.Visible = false
            end
        else
            DesyncDot.Visible = false
        end
        if SoulSettings.Desync.Status then
            DesyncStatus.Text = "Desync: ON (" .. mode .. ")"
            DesyncStatus.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
            DesyncStatus.Visible = true
        else
            DesyncStatus.Visible = false
        end
    else
        if BodyCloneDesync then BodyCloneDesync:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(CFrame.new(9999, 9999, 9999)) end
        DesyncLine.Visible = false
        DesyncDot.Visible = false
        DesyncStatus.Visible = false
    end
end)
local AntilockStatus = Drawing.new("Text")
AntilockStatus.Text = "Velocity Spoofer: OFF"
AntilockStatus.Size = 15
AntilockStatus.Font = 2
AntilockStatus.Outline = true
AntilockStatus.Position = Vector2.new(120, 140)
AntilockStatus.Visible = false
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if SoulSettings.Antilock.Enabled and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        local mode = SoulSettings.Antilock.Mode
        local oldV = hrp.Velocity
        if mode == "Prediction Breaker" then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        elseif mode == "Invert" then
            hrp.Velocity = -oldV
        elseif mode == "Random" then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-500, 500), math.random(-500, 500), math.random(-500, 500))
        elseif mode == "Zero" then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        elseif mode == "Custom" then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(SoulSettings.Antilock.CustomX, SoulSettings.Antilock.CustomY, SoulSettings.Antilock.CustomZ)
        elseif mode == "Sky" then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 999, 0)
        elseif mode == "Underground" then
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -999, 0)
        end
        if SoulSettings.Antilock.Status then
            AntilockStatus.Text = "Velocity Spoofer: ON (" .. mode .. ")"
            AntilockStatus.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
            AntilockStatus.Visible = true
        else
            AntilockStatus.Visible = false
        end
    else
        AntilockStatus.Visible = false
    end
end)
end
do
function Modules:InitGrab()
        DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("Grab")
        DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("GrabMovement")
        repeat task.wait() until LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") ~= nil 
        DhLibrary:AddConnection("Grab",Modules:GetChar("BodyEffects").Grabbed:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
            local GrabbedChar = Modules:GetChar("BodyEffects").Grabbed.Value 
            if GrabbedChar ~= nil then
                Modules:Delete(GrabbedChar,{"BodyVelocity","BodyGyro","BodyPosition"})
                Modules:Grab(GrabbedChar)
                DhLibrary:NoVelocity(GrabbedChar)
                DhLibrary:CanCollide(GrabbedChar,false)
                return 
            end 
            DhLibrary:StopAnimation(LocalPlayer.Character,3135389157)
        end))
    end 
    function Modules:Grab(char)
        assert(char ~= nil,"Character is nil")
        if not States.Grab then 
            DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("GrabMovement")
            return 
        end 
        States.GrabbedChar = char 
        States.LastGrabbed = char
        Modules:Delete(char,{"BodyVelocity","BodyGyro","BodyPosition"})
        DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Services.LocalPlayer.Character,11075367458)
        repeat task.wait() until char:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT")
        local GrabbingConstraint = char:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT")
        local Character  = Modules:GetChar("")
        local UpperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if not GrabbingConstraint:FindFirstChild("H")  or not Character then return end 
        local BodyPosition = DhLibrary:Create("BodyPosition",{
            D = 200,
            MaxForce = Vector3.new(10000, 10000, 10000),
            Position = Character:FindFirstChild("RightHand").Position - Vector3.new(0,1,0),
            Parent = UpperTorso
        })
        local BodyGyro = DhLibrary:Create("BodyGyro",{
            D = 100,
            MaxTorque = Vector3.new(10000, 10000, 10000),
            CFrame = CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position,Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position),
            Parent = UpperTorso
        })
        GrabbingConstraint.H.Length = 9e9 
        DhLibrary:PlayAudio(Options.CustomSound.Value or 4799902666) 
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(LocalPlayer.Character.LowerTorso:WaitForChild("BOOMBOXSOUND").TimeLength - 0.1)
            DhLibrary:StopAudio()
        end)
        DhLibrary:PlayAnimation(Services.LocalPlayer.Character,3135389157,0,.1)
        DhLibrary:AddConnection("GrabMovement",Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local Character = Modules:GetChar()
            local UpperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            if not States.Grab then 
                DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,3135389157)
                DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("GrabMovement")
                return 
            end  
            if States.GrabbedChar ~= nil 
            and Character ~= nil
             and Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") 
             and Modules:GetChar("BodyEffects").Grabbed.Value  ~= nil 
             and States.Grab and char.UpperTorso:FindFirstChild("BodyPosition") then  
                local Position = Character:FindFirstChild("RightHand").Position - Vector3.new(0,1,0) 
                if Options.grab_mode.Value == "Grab" then 
                    Position = Character:FindFirstChild("RightHand").Position - Vector3.new(0,1,0)
                    if not DhLibrary:IsAnimPlaying(Character,3135389157) then 
                        DhLibrary:PlayAnimation(Character,3135389157,0,.1)
                    end 
                elseif Options.grab_mode.Value == "Behind" then 
                    Position = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position - Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame.LookVector * 3
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,3135389157)
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,11075367458)
                elseif Options.grab_mode.Value == "Infront" then 
                    Position = Modules:GetForwardPosition(3)
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,3135389157)
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,11075367458)
                elseif Options.grab_mode.Value == "Custom" then 
                    Position = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position + Vector3.new(Options.xAxis.Value,Options.yAxis.Value,Options.zAxis.Value)
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,3135389157)
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,11075367458)
                end 
                local Orientation = CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position,Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position)
                if Options.grab_mode.Value == "Infront" then 
                    Orientation = CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position,Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position + Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame.LookVector * -5)
                else 
                    Orientation = CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position,Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position)
                end 
                BodyGyro.CFrame = Orientation
				BodyPosition.Position = Position
             elseif Modules:GetChar("BodyEffects"):FindFirstChild("Grabbed") and Modules:GetChar("BodyEffects").Grabbed.Value  == nil then 
                DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,3135389157)
                States.GrabbedChar = nil
            end 
        end))
    end 
    function Modules:DestroyTool(Text)
        local Tool = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(Text) or LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(Text)
        if Tool then 
            Tool:Destroy()
        end 
        DhLibrary:RemoveConnection(Text.."_tool")
    end 
    function Modules:GrabTool(options)
        local Character = Modules:GetChar()
        local Backpack = LocalPlayer.Backpack
        local tool_Callback = options.Callback 
        local name = options.name or options.Name 
        local description = options.Description
        local stopanimation = options.StopAnimations
        Tools[name] = {}
        local NewTool = DhLibrary:Create("Tool",{
            Name = name,
            RequiresHandle = false,
            ToolTip = description
        })
        DhLibrary:AddConnection(name.."_tool",NewTool.Activated:Connect(function()
            Tools[name]["State"] = true
            if not States.Grab or not States.GrabbedChar then 
                return 
            end
            States.Grab = false 
            local success,err = pcall(function()
                local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local GrabbedChar =  Character.BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("Grabbed").Value 
                if Character == nil then 
                    error("Localplayers character doesn't exist wth???")
                end 
                if GrabbedChar == nil then 
                    return 
                end 
                if stopanimation then 
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,3135389157)
                    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(Character,11075367458)
                end 
                tool_Callback(GrabbedChar,Character)
                NewTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                NewTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
            end)
            if not success then 
                print(err)
            end 
        end))
    end 
end 
local lastStompTime = 0
local STOMP_COOLDOWN = 2
local STOMP_EFFECT = "Thanos"
do 
    local function boobs(targetCharacter)
        STOMP_EFFECT = States.StompEffect or "Spirit"
        local currentTime = tick()
        if currentTime - lastStompTime < STOMP_COOLDOWN then 
            return 
        end
        lastStompTime = currentTime
        if STOMP_EFFECT == "RoadRoller" then 
            RoadRoller.Parent = workspace
            RoadRoller.Anchored = true 
            RoadRoller.CFrame = targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 40, 0)
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://4877348549"
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
            Sound:Destroy()    
            TweenService:Create(
                RoadRoller,
                TweenInfo.new(0.2), 
                { 
                    CFrame = targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * 
                            CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) * 
                            CFrame.Angles(math.rad(200), -math.rad(200), math.rad(90)) 
                }
            ):Play()
            ShakeModule.StartShake(7, 0.5)
            task.wait(2)
            RoadRoller.CFrame = CFrame.new(20,2000,20)
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Rings" then 
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://7456436580"
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
            Sound:Destroy()
            local FakePart = Instance.new("Part",workspace)
            FakePart.Transparency = 1
            FakePart.Position = targetCharacter.Head.Position
            FakePart.Anchored = true 
            FakePart.CanCollide = false 
            SonicRings.Parent = FakePart
            task.wait(0.5)
            SonicRings.Parent = workspace
            FakePart:Destroy()
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "BlackHole" then 
            BlackHole.Parent = workspace
            BlackHole.Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position + Vector3.new(0,1,0)
            BlackHole.open:Play()
            ShakeModule.StartShake(7, 0.5)
            for _, emitter in pairs(BlackHole:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or emitter:IsA("Beam") then 
                    emitter.Enabled = true  
                end 
            end 
            task.wait(1.5)
            task.spawn(function()
                BlackHole.wind:Play()
                BlackHole.bring:Play()
                for _, part in pairs(targetCharacter:GetChildren()) do 
                    if not part:IsA("BasePart") then  end 
                    TweenService:Create(part,TweenInfo.new(1.4),{
                        Position = BlackHole.Event.WorldPosition
                    }):Play()
                end 
                task.wait(1.5)
                targetCharacter:Destroy()
            end)
            task.wait(3)
            TweenService:Create(BlackHole.wind,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                Volume = 0
            }):Play()
            for _, emitter in pairs(BlackHole:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or emitter:IsA("Beam") then 
                    emitter.Enabled = false 
                end 
            end 
            task.wait(2)
            BlackHole.Parent = Services.ReplicatedStorage
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Spirit" then 
            Spirit.Stomp:Play()
            Aura.Transparency = 0
            Aura.Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position 
            ShakeModule.StartShake(7, 0.5)
            TweenService:Create(Aura,TweenInfo.new(2),{
                Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position + Vector3.new(0,50,0),
                Size = Vector3.new(120, 90, 120),
                Transparency = 1
            }):Play()
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://6290067239"
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
            Sound:Destroy()
            for _, asset in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do 
                if not asset:IsA("BasePart") or not asset:IsA("Decal") then  end 
                TweenService:Create(asset,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                    Transparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end 
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Charm" then 
            local Stomp = Charm.FX.Stomp 
            local Sound = Charm.SFX:FindFirstChild("Explosive Hit 5")
            local TargetPos = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position
            local Emitters = {}
            local PartOne = Stomp.PartOne:Clone()
            local Part    = Stomp.Part:Clone()
            Charm.Parent = workspace
            PartOne.Position = TargetPos
            PartOne.Anchored = true 
            PartOne.Parent = workspace
            Part.Position = TargetPos
            Part.Anchored = true 
            Part.Parent = workspace
            for _, emitter in pairs(PartOne:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                    table.insert(Emitters,emitter)
                end 
            end 
            for _, emitter in pairs(Part:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                    table.insert(Emitters,emitter)
                end 
            end
            for _, emitter in pairs(Emitters) do 
                emitter.Enabled = true   
            end 
            local old_color = LightingService.FogColor
            Sound:Play()
            for _, asset in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do 
                if not asset:IsA("BasePart") or not asset:IsA("Decal") then  end 
                TweenService:Create(asset,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                    Transparency = 1
                }):Play()
            end 
            TweenService:Create(LightingService,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 42, 191)
            }):Play()
            task.wait(0.8)
            Part:Destroy()
            PartOne:Destroy()
            TweenService:Create(LightingService,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                FogColor = old_color
            }):Play()
            Charm.Parent = Services.ReplicatedStorage
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Thanos" then
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",workspace)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://3050376525"
            Sound:Play()
            for _, part in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do 
                if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("MeshPart") then 
                    local Thanos_Effect = Thanos:Clone() 
                    Thanos_Effect.Parent = part 
                    Thanos_Effect.Color =  ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, part.Color), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, Color3.fromRGB(65, 33, 18)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)) })
                    TweenService:Create(part,TweenInfo.new(0.5),{
                        Transparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                    task.delay(0.5,function()
                        Thanos_Effect.Enabled = false 
                    end)
                    task.wait(.1)
                end 
            end
            repeat task.wait() until Sound.TimeLength == 0
            Sound:Destroy()
        elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Afterslash" then 
            local AfterSlashEfx = AfterSlash:Clone()
            local UpperTorso = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            UpperTorso.Position = (CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position) * CFrame.new(0,35,0)).Position
            local Part = AfterSlashEfx.Part
            Part.Position = UpperTorso.Position
            Part.Parent = UpperTorso
            Part.Size = Vector3.new(5,7,5)
            for _ = 1, 25 do 
                task.wait(math.random(1,9) / 100)
                local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",Part)
                Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://5989945551"
                local DistortionSoundEffect = Instance.new("DistortionSoundEffect",Sound)
                Sound.PlayOnRemove = true 
                Sound:Destroy()
                Part.Position = UpperTorso.Position
            end
            UpperTorso.Position = (CFrame.new(UpperTorso.Position) * CFrame.new(0, -5, 0)).Position
            for _, emitter in pairs(Part:GetDescendants()) do
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                    emitter.Enabled = false
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
            local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment",workspace.Terrain)
            Attachment.WorldPosition = UpperTorso.Position
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound",Part)
            Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://4471648128" 
            Sound.PlayOnRemove = true
            Sound:Destroy()
            for _, emitter in pairs(AfterSlashEfx.Burst:GetDescendants()) do 
                if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                    emitter.Parent = Attachment
                    emitter:Emit(emitter:GetAttribute("EmitCount"))
                end 
            end 
            for _, part in pairs(targetCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    TweenService:Create(part, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {
                        Transparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                end
            end 
        end 
    end 
    function Modules:StompEffects(state)
        if not state then 
            DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("StompEffect")
            return 
        end 
        DhLibrary:AddConnection("StompEffect", RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local playerCharacter = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not playerCharacter or not playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or not playerCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") then
                return
            end
            local rayOrigin = playerCharacter.LowerTorso.Position
            local rayDirection = Vector3.new(0, -playerCharacter.UpperTorso.Size.y * 4.5, 0)
            local whitelist = {}
            for _, player in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= Services.LocalPlayer and player.Character and not  whitelist[player.Character] then
                    table.insert(whitelist, player.Character)
                end
            end
            if #whitelist > 0 then
                local hitPart, hitPosition, hitNormal = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithWhitelist(
                    Ray.new(rayOrigin, rayDirection),
                    whitelist,
                    false,
                    true
                )
                if hitPart then
                    local stompedCharacter = hitPart:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    if stompedCharacter and stompedCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and stompedCharacter:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") and stompedCharacter.BodyEffects.SDeath.Value and DhLibrary:IsAnimPlaying(playerCharacter,2816431506) then
                        boobs(stompedCharacter)
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
    end 
end 
do 
    function Modules:ImpactFrame()
        local Black = DhLibrary:Create("ColorCorrectionEffect",{
            TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0),
        })
        local White = DhLibrary:Create("Highlight",{
            FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
            FillTransparency = 0
        })
        task.wait(.1)
        Black.Parent = LightingService
        White.Parent = Modules:GetCharacter("")
        task.wait(.1)
        White.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
        Black.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        Black.Brightness = 1
        task.wait(.1)
        White:Destroy()
        Black:Destroy()
    end 
    function Modules:CloneChar(Character)
        local LocalChar = Modules:GetChar("")
        LocalChar.Archivable = true 
        local ClonedChar = LocalChar:Clone() 
        ClonedChar.Parent = Services.Workspace
        ClonedChar.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0,2,0)
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
        if ClonedChar:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then 
            ClonedChar:FindFirstChild("ForceField"):Destroy()
        end 
        local Player = Services.Players:FindFirstChild(Character.Name)
        if not Player then 
            return 
        end 
        local HumanoidDescription = Services.Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(Player.UserId)
        ClonedChar.Humanoid:ApplyDescription(HumanoidDescription)
        LocalChar.Archivable = false
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.BodyWidthScale.Value
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.BodyProportionScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.BodyProportionScale.Value
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.BodyDepthScale.Value
        ClonedChar.Humanoid.HeadScale.Value = Character.Humanoid.HeadScale.Value
        for _, accessory in pairs(ClonedChar:GetDescendants()) do 
            if accessory:IsA("Accessory") or accessory:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or accessory:IsA("Shirt") or accessory:IsA("Pants") then
                accessory:Destroy()
            end
            if accessory:IsA("BasePart") then 
                accessory.Transparency = 1
            end 
        end
        return ClonedChar 
    end 
    function Modules:AlignControl(part, part1, Offset)
        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        part.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        part.CFrame = part1.CFrame * (Offset or CFrame.new())
        part.CanCollide = false
        part1.CanCollide = false
    end
end

local function applyCustomAnimations(character)
    if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("Animate") then return end
    local Animate = character.Animate
    local Cloned = Animate:Clone()
    Cloned.idle.Animation1.AnimationId = AnimationOptions.Idle1
    Cloned.idle.Animation2.AnimationId = AnimationOptions.Idle2
    Cloned.walk.WalkAnim.AnimationId   = AnimationOptions.Walk
    Cloned.run.RunAnim.AnimationId     = AnimationOptions.Run
    Cloned.jump.JumpAnim.AnimationId   = AnimationOptions.Jump
    Cloned.climb.ClimbAnim.AnimationId = AnimationOptions.Climb
    Cloned.fall.FallAnim.AnimationId   = AnimationOptions.Fall
    Animate:Destroy()
    Cloned.Parent = character
end
localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    if KeepOnDeath then
        task.wait(1.5)
        applyCustomAnimations(char)
    end
end)
local animPackNames = {}
for name,_ in pairs(AnimationSets) do table.insert(animPackNames, name) end
table.sort(animPackNames)
local function updateAnimation(key, id)
    AnimationOptions[key] = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=" .. id
    if localPlayer.Character then
        applyCustomAnimations(localPlayer.Character)
    end
end
end

local danceTrack = nil
local function loadDance(name)
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then return end
    if danceTrack then
        danceTrack:Stop()
        danceTrack:Destroy()
        danceTrack = nil
    end
    local animId = DanceList[name]
    if animId then
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. animId
        danceTrack = char.Humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
        danceTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
        if getgenv().SoulTech.Dance.Playing then
            danceTrack.Looped = true
            danceTrack:Play()
        end
    end
end
end

        Cloned.idle.Animation2.AnimationId = AnimationOptions.Idle2

function Modules:Void(character)
    if not character or not character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -500, 0)
end
function Modules:InitGrab()
    DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("Grab")
    DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("GrabMovement")
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local be = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
    if not be or not be:FindFirstChild("Grabbed") then return end
    DhLibrary:AddConnection("Grab", be.Grabbed:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(function()
        local grabbedChar = be.Grabbed.Value
        if grabbedChar ~= nil then
            Modules:Delete(grabbedChar, { "BodyVelocity", "BodyGyro", "BodyPosition" })
            Modules:Grab(grabbedChar)
            DhLibrary:NoVelocity(grabbedChar)
            DhLibrary:CanCollide(grabbedChar, false)
            return
        end
        DhLibrary:StopAnimation(LocalPlayer.Character, 3135389157)
    end))
end
function Modules:Grab(char)
    if not char then return end
    if not States.Grab then
        DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("GrabMovement")
        return
    end
    States.GrabbedChar = char
    States.LastGrabbed = char
    Modules:Delete(char, { "BodyVelocity", "BodyGyro", "BodyPosition" })
    DhLibrary:StopAnimation(LocalPlayer.Character, 11075367458)
    local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local enemyHRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and enemyHRP then
        DhLibrary:AddConnection("GrabMovement", RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if States.Grab and States.GrabbedChar == char and hrp and enemyHRP then
                local mode = SoulSettings.Trolling.GrabMode
                local offset = CFrame.new(SoulSettings.Trolling.AxisX, SoulSettings.Trolling.AxisY, SoulSettings.Trolling.AxisZ)
                if mode == "Neckgrab" then
                    offset = CFrame.new(0, 0, -2) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
                elseif mode == "Up" or mode == "HeavenThrow" then
                    offset = CFrame.new(0, 15, 0)
                elseif mode == "Air" then
                    offset = CFrame.new(0, 30, 0)
                elseif mode == "Orbit" then
                    local a = tick() * 5
                    offset = CFrame.new(math.cos(a) * 5, 0, math.sin(a) * 5)
                end
                enemyHRP.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * offset
                enemyHRP.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end))
    end
end
function Modules:GrabTool(options)
    local name = options.name or options.Name
    local desc = options.Description or ""
    local cb = options.Callback
    local tool = DhLibrary:Create("Tool", {
        Name = name,
        RequiresHandle = false,
        ToolTip = desc
    })
    DhLibrary:AddConnection(name .. "_tool", tool.Activated:Connect(function()
        if not States.Grab or not States.GrabbedChar then return end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        local grabbed = char and char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") and char.BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("Grabbed") and char.BodyEffects.Grabbed.Value
        if grabbed and cb then
            cb(grabbed, char)
        end
    end))
    pcall(function() tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack end)
    Tools[name] = tool
    return tool
end
local function executeTrollingAction(actionName, targetPlr)
    if not targetPlr or not targetPlr.Character then return end
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local targetChar = targetPlr.Character
    if not myChar or not targetChar then return end
    local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local targetHRP = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHRP or not targetHRP then return end
    if actionName == "Bang" or actionName == "Rape" then
        task.spawn(function()
            local startT = tick()
            while tick() - startT < 5 and myHRP and targetHRP do
                myHRP.CFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 1.2)
                DhLibrary:PlayAnimation(myChar, 3152378852)
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
    elseif actionName == "Kiss" then
        myHRP.CFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -1) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
    elseif actionName == "Control Body" then
        for _, part in pairs(targetChar:GetChildren()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and myChar:FindFirstChild(part.Name) then
                DhLibrary:AlignControl(part, myChar[part.Name], CFrame.new())
            end
        end
    elseif actionName == "Kick" then
        DhLibrary:PlayAnimation(myChar, 2816431506)
        targetHRP.Velocity = (targetHRP.Position - myHRP.Position).Unit * 200 + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0)
    end
end
end

local function applyCustomSkybox(name)
    if customSkyInstance then
        customSkyInstance:Destroy()
    end
    customSkyInstance = Instance.new("Sky")
    local sky = SkyboxAssets[name]
    customSkyInstance.SkyboxBk = sky.Bk
    customSkyInstance.SkyboxDn = sky.Dn
    customSkyInstance.SkyboxFt = sky.Ft
    customSkyInstance.SkyboxLf = sky.Lf
    customSkyInstance.SkyboxRt = sky.Rt
    customSkyInstance.SkyboxUp = sky.Up
    customSkyInstance.Name = "CustomSky"
    customSkyInstance.Parent = lighting
end
local function restoreDefaultSkybox()
    if customSkyInstance then
        customSkyInstance:Destroy()
        customSkyInstance = nil
    end
    if DefaultSky then
        DefaultSky.SkyboxBk = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxBk
        DefaultSky.SkyboxDn = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxDn
        DefaultSky.SkyboxFt = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxFt
        DefaultSky.SkyboxLf = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxLf
        DefaultSky.SkyboxRt = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxRt
        DefaultSky.SkyboxUp = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxUp
        DefaultSky.Parent = lighting
    end
end
end

local function applySkybox(name)
    local sky = SkyboxAssets[name]
    if not sky then return end
    local customSky = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SoulTech_Skybox") or Instance.new("Sky")
    customSky.Name = "SoulTech_Skybox"
    customSky.SkyboxBk = sky.Bk
    customSky.SkyboxDn = sky.Dn
    customSky.SkyboxFt = sky.Ft
    customSky.SkyboxLf = sky.Lf
    customSky.SkyboxRt = sky.Rt
    customSky.SkyboxUp = sky.Up
    customSky.Parent = Lighting
end
local function restoreLighting()
    local customSky = Lighting:FindFirstChild("SoulTech_Skybox")
    if customSky then customSky:Destroy() end
end
local function updateWorldLighting()
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomAmbient then
        Lighting.Ambient = SoulSettings.World.AmbientColor
    end
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomOutdoorAmbient then
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = SoulSettings.World.OutdoorAmbientColor
    end
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomFog then
        Lighting.FogColor = SoulSettings.World.FogColor
        Lighting.FogStart = SoulSettings.World.FogStart
        Lighting.FogEnd = SoulSettings.World.FogEnd
    end
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomBrightness then
        Lighting.Brightness = SoulSettings.World.BrightnessValue
    end
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomClockTime then
        Lighting.ClockTime = SoulSettings.World.ClockTimeValue
    end
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = SoulSettings.World.GlobalShadows
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomExposure then
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = SoulSettings.World.ExposureValue
    end
    if SoulSettings.World.CustomSkyboxEnabled then
        applySkybox(SoulSettings.World.SkyboxSelected)
    else
        restoreLighting()
    end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(updateWorldLighting)
local modList = {
    163721789, 15427717, 201454243, 822999, 63794379, 17260230, 28357488, 93101606,
    8195210, 89473551, 16917269, 85989579, 1553950697, 476537893, 155627580,

    4255947062, 29242182, 2395613299, 3314981799, 3390225662, 2459178,
    2846299656, 2967502742, 7001683347, 7312775547, 328566086, 170526279,
    99356639, 352087139, 6074834798, 2212830051, 3944434729, 5136267958,
    84570351, 542488819, 1830168970, 3950637598, 1962396833
}
local groupIDs = { 10604500, 17215700 }
local function monitorStaff()
    if not SoulSettings.Misc.AntiMod then return end
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player == LocalPlayer then  end
        if table.find(modList, player.UserId) then
            local msg = "STAFF DETECTED: " .. player.DisplayName .. " (@" .. player.Name .. ")"
            if SoulSettings.Misc.AntiModMethod == "Notify" then
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Soul.Tech Security", Text = msg, Duration = 6 })
            else
                LocalPlayer:Kick("MOD DETECTED: " .. player.DisplayName)
            end
        end
        if SoulSettings.Misc.GroupCheck then
            for _, gid in ipairs(groupIDs) do
                local success, inGroup = pcall(player.IsInGroup, player, gid)
                if success and inGroup then
                    local msg = "STAFF GROUP MEMBER: " .. player.DisplayName
                    if SoulSettings.Misc.AntiModMethod == "Notify" then
                        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Soul.Tech Security", Text = msg, Duration = 6 })
                    else
                        LocalPlayer:Kick("STAFF IN GAME: " .. player.DisplayName)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(3)
        pcall(monitorStaff)
    end
end)
local flashbackHistory = {}
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        table.insert(flashbackHistory, 1, hrp.CFrame)
        if #flashbackHistory > 100 then
            table.remove(flashbackHistory)
        end
    end
end)
local function triggerFlashback()
    if #flashbackHistory > 0 and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        local targetCF = flashbackHistory[math.min(50, #flashbackHistory)]
        hrp.CFrame = targetCF
    end
end
local lastSafeCFrame = CFrame.new(0, 50, 0)
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and hrp.Position.Y > -20 then
            lastSafeCFrame = hrp.CFrame
        end
        if SoulSettings.Misc.AntiVoid and hrp.Position.Y < -100 then
            hrp.CFrame = lastSafeCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
            hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if SoulSettings.Misc.AntiBag then
            for _, obj in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                if obj.Name == "Christmas_Bag" or string.find(obj.Name, "Bag") then
                    pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
        if SoulSettings.Misc.AntiGrab then
            local be = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
            if be and be:FindFirstChild("Grabbed") and be.Grabbed.Value ~= nil then
                local grabConstraint = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT")
                if grabConstraint then pcall(function() grabConstraint:Destroy() end) end
            end
        end
        if SoulSettings.Misc.InfZoom then
            LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = 1e5
        end
    end
end)

local function sendChatMessage(msg)
    pcall(function()
        local chatRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents") and ReplicatedStorage.DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
        if chatRemote then
            chatRemote:FireServer(msg, "All")
        else
            local tc = game:GetService("TextChatService").TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
            if tc then tc:SendAsync(msg) end
        end
    end)
end
end

local AnimGroup = Tabs.Animations:AddLeftGroupbox("Animation Packs")
local packList = {}
for name, _ in pairs(AnimationSets) do table.insert(packList, name) end
table.sort(packList)
AnimGroup:AddDropdown("AnimPack", {
    Values = packList,
    Default = 1,
    Multi = false,
    Text = "Animation Pack Choice",
    Callback = function(v)
        local set = AnimationSets[v]
        if set then
            updateAnimation("Idle1", set.idle1)
            updateAnimation("Idle2", set.idle2)
            updateAnimation("Walk", set.walk)
            updateAnimation("Run", set.run)
            updateAnimation("Jump", set.jump)
            updateAnimation("Climb", set.climb)
            updateAnimation("Fall", set.fall)
        end
    end
})

local AutoChatGroup = Tabs.AutoChat:AddLeftGroupbox("Auto Chatters")
local isRizzChatting = false
AutoChatGroup:AddToggle("RizzChatToggle", {
    Text = "Rizz Chatter",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        isRizzChatting = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while isRizzChatting do
                    local msg = RizzLines[math.random(1, #RizzLines)]
                    sendChatMessage(msg)
                    task.wait(4)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})
local isToxicChatting = false
AutoChatGroup:AddToggle("ToxicChatToggle", {
    Text = "Toxic Chatter",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        isToxicChatting = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while isToxicChatting do
                    local msg = ToxicLines[math.random(1, #ToxicLines)]
                    sendChatMessage(msg)
                    task.wait(4)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})
local isPromoChatting = false
AutoChatGroup:AddToggle("PromoChatToggle", {
    Text = "Promo Chatter",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        isPromoChatting = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                while isPromoChatting do
                    local msg = PromoLines[math.random(1, #PromoLines)]
                    sendChatMessage(msg)
                    task.wait(4)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})
local MenuGroup = Tabs['UI Settings']:AddLeftGroupbox("Menu Settings")
MenuGroup:AddButton("Unload Soul.Tech", function()
    pcall(function() Library:Unload() end)
    getgenv().SoulTechLoaded = nil
end)
MenuGroup:AddButton("Rejoin Server", function()
    TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
end)
MenuGroup:AddButton("Server Hop", function()
    pcall(function()
        local servers = HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. tostring(PlaceId) .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")).data
        for _, s in ipairs(servers) do
            if s.playing < s.maxPlayers and s.id ~= game.JobId then
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PlaceId, s.id, LocalPlayer)
                break
            end
        end
    end)
end)
ThemeManager:SetLibrary(Library)
SaveManager:SetLibrary(Library)
SaveManager:IgnoreThemeSettings()
SaveManager:SetIgnoreIndexes({ "MenuKeybind" })
ThemeManager:SetFolder("SoulTech")
SaveManager:SetFolder("SoulTech/configs")
SaveManager:BuildConfigSection(Tabs['UI Settings'])
ThemeManager:ApplyToTab(Tabs['UI Settings'])
Notify("Soul.Tech Universal loaded successfully!", 4)
